<script id="deen-v1072-onboarding-atlas-loader-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV1072AtlasLoader)return;
 window.__deenV1072AtlasLoader=true;
 const VERSION='9171onboardingatlasasset1';
 const base='./release/v9.9.3-direct/assets/onboarding-hq/atlas.part';
 const urls=[0,1,2,3,4].map(i=>base+i+'.txt?v='+VERSION);
 async function load(){
   const parts=await Promise.all(urls.map(async u=>{
     const r=await fetch(u,{cache:'no-store'});
     if(!r.ok)throw new Error('atlas HTTP '+r.status);
     return (await r.text()).trim();
   }));
   const b64=parts.join('');
   if(b64.length!==98636)throw new Error('atlas length '+b64.length);
   const src='data:image/webp;base64,'+b64;
   const test=new Image();
   await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
     test.onload=resolve;
     test.onerror=()=>reject(new Error('atlas decode'));
     test.src=src;
   });
   let style=document.getElementById('deen-v1072-onboarding-atlas-image-css');
   if(!style){
     style=document.createElement('style');
     style.id='deen-v1072-onboarding-atlas-image-css';
     document.head.appendChild(style);
   }
   style.textContent='#v998Onboarding[data-step="3"] .v998-animal:before{background-image:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(2,17,23,.02) 44%,rgba(3,25,32,.90) 100%),url("'+src+'")!important}';
   const mark=()=>{
     const root=document.getElementById('v998Onboarding');
     if(root?.dataset?.step==='3')root.dataset.hqAtlasReady='1';
   };
   mark();
   new MutationObserver(mark).observe(document.documentElement,{childList:true,subtree:true});
   window.DEEN_ONBOARDING_ATLAS_HOTFIX={
     version:'10.7.2',
     ready:true,
     bytes:73976,
     b64:b64.length,
     verify:()=>({
       rootStep:document.getElementById('v998Onboarding')?.dataset?.step||null,
       ready:document.getElementById('v998Onboarding')?.dataset?.hqAtlasReady||'0',
       imageStyle:!!document.getElementById('deen-v1072-onboarding-atlas-image-css'),
       cards:document.querySelectorAll('#v998Onboarding[data-step="3"] .v998-animal').length
     })
   };
 }
 load().catch(err=>{
   console.error('DEEN HQ atlas load failed',err);
   window.DEEN_ONBOARDING_ATLAS_HOTFIX={
     version:'10.7.2',
     ready:false,
     error:String(err?.message||err)
   };
 });
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v1077-profile-avatar-assets-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.__deenV1077ProfileAvatarAssets)return;
 window.__deenV1077ProfileAvatarAssets=true;

 const VERSION='10.7.7';
 const ASSETS={
   lion:'./assets/companions/lion/lion_avatar.png',
   wolf:'./assets/companions/wolf/wolf_avatar.png',
   owl:'./assets/companions/owl/owl_avatar.png',
   turtle:'./assets/companions/turtle/turtle_avatar.png',
   eagle:'./assets/companions/eagle/eagle_avatar.png',
   cat:'./assets/companions/cat/cat_avatar.png'
 };
 const LABELS={
   lion:'Aslan',
   wolf:'Kurt',
   owl:'Baykuş',
   turtle:'Kaplumbağa',
   eagle:'Kartal',
   cat:'Kedi'
 };

 function selected(){
   try{
     const id=String(state?.profile?.companion||'').trim().toLowerCase();
     return ASSETS[id]?id:'';
   }catch(_){return ''}
 }

 function apply(){
   const root=document.getElementById('profileScreenBody');
   const avatar=root?.querySelector('.v5-profilehead .v5-avatar.large');
   const id=selected();
   if(!avatar||!id)return false;

   const wanted=ASSETS[id];
   const existing=avatar.querySelector('.v1077-profile-mascot');
   if(existing&&existing.dataset.animal===id&&existing.getAttribute('src')?.includes(wanted)){
     return true;
   }

   avatar.classList.add('v994-companion-avatar','v1077-profile-avatar');
   avatar.innerHTML='';

   const img=document.createElement('img');
   img.className='v1077-profile-mascot';
   img.dataset.animal=id;
   img.src=wanted+'?v=9178profileavatarrestore1';
   img.alt='';
   img.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
   avatar.appendChild(img);

   const label=LABELS[id]||id;
   avatar.title=label;
   avatar.setAttribute('aria-label','Yol arkadaşın '+label+'. Değiştirmek için dokun.');
   if(typeof window.DEEN_COMPANION?.open==='function'){
     avatar.onclick=()=>window.DEEN_COMPANION.open('profile');
   }

   avatar.dataset.mascotAsset='original-avatar';
   avatar.dataset.animal=id;
   document.documentElement.dataset.deenProfileMascot=id;
   return true;
 }

 function schedule(){
   requestAnimationFrame(apply);
   setTimeout(apply,40);
   setTimeout(apply,120);
 }

 document.addEventListener('deen:profile-rendered',schedule);
 document.addEventListener('deen:ui-updated',schedule);
 document.addEventListener('deen:navigated',schedule);

 const body=document.getElementById('profileScreenBody');
 if(body)new MutationObserver(schedule).observe(body,{childList:true,subtree:true});

 const baseSelect=window.DEEN_COMPANION?.select;
 if(typeof baseSelect==='function'&&!baseSelect.__deenV1077Wrapped){
   const wrapped=function(){
     const out=baseSelect.apply(this,arguments);
     schedule();
     return out;
   };
   wrapped.__deenV1077Wrapped=true;
   window.DEEN_COMPANION.select=wrapped;
 }

 window.DEEN_PROFILE_AVATAR_ASSETS={
   version:VERSION,
   assets:{...ASSETS},
   apply,
   check:()=>({
     selected:selected(),
     src:document.querySelector('#profileScreenBody .v1077-profile-mascot')?.getAttribute('src')||null,
     rendered:document.querySelector('#profileScreenBody .v1077-profile-mascot')?.dataset?.animal||null
   })
 };

 schedule();
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v1088-legacy-neon-persist-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV1088LegacyNeonPersist)return;
  window.__deenV1088LegacyNeonPersist=true;

  const VERSION='10.8.8';
  const BASE_PREVIEW=typeof window.preview==='function'?window.preview:null;

  const safeEsc=x=>{
    try{return typeof esc==='function'?esc(String(x??'')):String(x??'')}catch(_){return String(x??'')}
  };

  function stageIcon(kind,done,locked){
    if(locked)return '<path d="M8 11V8a4 4 0 0 1 8 0v3"/><rect x="6" y="11" width="12" height="9" rx="2"/>';
    if(done)return '<path d="m5 12 4 4L19 6"/>';
    if(kind==='challenge')return '<path d="m12 3 2.5 5.3L20 9l-4 3.9.9 5.6L12 16l-4.9 2.5.9-5.6L4 9l5.5-.7L12 3Z"/>';
    if(kind==='review')return '<path d="M20 7v5h-5"/><path d="M19 12a7 7 0 1 1-2-5"/>';
    return '<path d="M5 5.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 7.5 3H12v17H7.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 5 22V5.5Z"/><path d="M19 5.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 0 16.5 3H12v17h4.5A2.5 2.5 0 0 1 19 22V5.5Z"/>';
  }

  function neonPreview(id){
    try{
      const stg=STAGES.find(x=>x.id===id);
      if(!stg)return BASE_PREVIEW?.(id);

      const unit=stg.unit;
      const stages=unitStages(unit);
      const index=stages.indexOf(stg);
      const status=stageState(unit,index);
      const qs=chooseQuestions(id);
      const due=poolFor(id).filter(q=>state.reviewSchedule[q.id]?.nextReview<=Date.now()).length;
      const desc=DESC[Number(unit.slice(1))-1]||'';
      const mastery=masteryFor(unit);
      const label=stg.kind==='challenge'?'USTALIK':stg.kind==='review'?'PEKİŞTİRME':'KISA DERS';
      const accent=stg.kind==='challenge'?'#d9bc79':stg.kind==='review'?'#8d79ff':'#bc35ed';
      const soft=stg.kind==='challenge'?'#f1dca6':stg.kind==='review'?'#d9d2ff':'#efd0ff';
      const locked=!!status.locked;
      const done=!!status.done;
      const reason=Number(unit.slice(1))>=6&&!state.sectionFinal.completed
        ?'Kısım 1 · Temeller Ustalığı finalini geçmelisin.'
        :index>0
          ?'Önce “'+safeEsc(stages[index-1].title)+'” aşamasını tamamla.'
          :'Önce bir önceki ünitenin son aşamasını tamamla.';

      const action=locked
        ?'<div class="deen-intro-status">⌑ '+reason+'</div>'
        :!qs.length
          ?'<div class="deen-intro-status">Doğrulanmış uygun soru henüz bağlı değil. Enerji harcanmaz.</div>'
          :'<button class="v5-primary" type="button" onclick="startStage(\''+safeEsc(id)+'\')">'+(done?'TEKRAR BAŞLA':'BAŞLA')+'</button>';

      const reinforce=due>=2&&!locked
        ?'<button class="v5-listbtn" type="button" onclick="startStage(\''+safeEsc(id)+'\')"><span>↻ Bu konuyu pekiştir</span><b>→</b></button>'
        :'';

      const body=
        '<div class="deen-stage-intro" style="--deen-intro-accent:'+accent+';--deen-intro-soft:'+soft+'">'+
          '<button class="deen-intro-back" type="button" onclick="closeSheet()"><span>‹</span> Öğrenme yoluna dön</button>'+
          '<div class="deen-intro-art">'+
            '<span class="deen-intro-halo"></span>'+
            '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.7" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">'+stageIcon(stg.kind,done,locked)+'</svg>'+
            '<i class="deen-intro-spark one">✦</i><i class="deen-intro-spark two">✧</i>'+
          '</div>'+
          '<div class="deen-intro-kicker">ÜNİTE '+Number(unit.slice(1))+' · AŞAMA '+(index+1)+' · '+label+'</div>'+
          '<div class="deen-intro-title">'+safeEsc(stg.title)+'</div>'+
          '<p class="deen-intro-desc">'+safeEsc(desc)+'</p>'+
          '<div class="deen-intro-goal"><span>HEDEF</span><b>'+qs.length+' soru · '+stg.energy+' enerji · +'+stg.xp+' baz XP · Ustalık %'+mastery+'</b></div>'+
          '<div class="deen-intro-reward"><span>✦</span> Yaklaşık '+(stg.kind==='challenge'?5:3)+' dakika</div>'+
          action+reinforce+
          '<div class="deen-intro-status">Gün içindeki tekrarlarında XP azalır.</div>'+
        '</div>';

      sheet(locked?'Bu ders henüz kilitli':stg.title,body);
      ensureDialog();
      return true;
    }catch(err){
      console.warn('Legacy neon preview fallback',err);
      return BASE_PREVIEW?.(id);
    }
  }

  function ensureDialog(){
    const d=document.getElementById('v5Sheet');
    if(!d)return false;
    const hasIntro=!!d.querySelector('.deen-stage-intro');
    if(hasIntro&&!d.classList.contains('deen-intro-dialog')){
      d.classList.add('deen-intro-dialog');
    }
    return hasIntro;
  }

  window.preview=neonPreview;
  try{preview=neonPreview}catch(_){}

  const d=document.getElementById('v5Sheet');
  if(d&&'MutationObserver' in window){
    new MutationObserver(()=>{
      if(d.querySelector('.deen-stage-intro')&&!d.classList.contains('deen-intro-dialog')){
        d.classList.add('deen-intro-dialog');
      }
    }).observe(d,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }

  document.addEventListener('deen:path-rendered',()=>{
    document.documentElement.dataset.deenLegacyNeon='persistent';
  });

  window.DEEN_LEGACY_NEON={
    version:VERSION,
    preview:neonPreview,
    ensureDialog,
    check:()=>({
      current:document.querySelectorAll('.restored-path.deen-old-path .lp-node.current').length,
      intro:!!document.querySelector('#v5Sheet.deen-intro-dialog .deen-stage-intro'),
      css:!!document.getElementById('deen-v1088-legacy-neon-persist-css')
    })
  };

  document.documentElement.dataset.deenLegacyNeon='persistent';
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v1089-multilingual-typography-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV1089Typography)return;
  window.__deenV1089Typography=true;

  const RTL=new Set(['ar','fa','ur','he']);
  const normalize=locale=>String(locale||'tr').trim().toLowerCase().replace('_','-');

  function setLocale(locale){
    const value=normalize(locale);
    const base=value.split('-')[0]||'tr';
    document.documentElement.lang=value;
    document.documentElement.dir=RTL.has(base)?'rtl':'ltr';
    document.documentElement.dataset.deenLocale=value;
    return {locale:value,dir:document.documentElement.dir};
  }

  const current=normalize(document.documentElement.lang||'tr');
  if(!document.documentElement.dir){
    document.documentElement.dir=RTL.has(current.split('-')[0])?'rtl':'ltr';
  }
  document.documentElement.dataset.deenTypography='multilingual-v1';

  if(document.fonts?.ready){
    document.fonts.ready.then(()=>{
      document.documentElement.dataset.deenFontsReady='1';
    }).catch(()=>{});
  }

  window.DEEN_TYPOGRAPHY={
    version:'10.8.9',
    build:'9190multitype1',
    primary:'Nunito Sans',
    fallback:'Noto Sans',
    arabic:'Noto Sans Arabic',
    setLocale,
    check:()=>({
      locale:document.documentElement.lang,
      dir:document.documentElement.dir,
      font:getComputedStyle(document.body).fontFamily,
      ready:document.documentElement.dataset.deenFontsReady||'0',
      css:!!document.getElementById('deen-v1089-multilingual-typography-css')
    })
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v1091-typography-freeze-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV1091TypographyFreeze)return;
  window.__deenV1091TypographyFreeze=true;

  const IDS=[
    'deen-v1089-multilingual-typography-css',
    'deen-v1090-readability-guard-css',
    'deen-v1091-typography-freeze-css',
    'deen-v1092-luxury-neon-home-css'
  ];

  function enforce(){
    const head=document.head;
    if(!head)return false;

    const nodes=IDS.map(id=>document.getElementById(id)).filter(Boolean);
    if(!nodes.length)return false;

    // Only reorder when necessary. Never keep observing <head>;
    // the previous observer could create an append -> mutation -> append loop on mobile.
    let lastIndex=-1;
    let inOrder=true;
    const children=[...head.children];
    for(const node of nodes){
      const idx=children.indexOf(node);
      if(idx<=lastIndex){inOrder=false;break;}
      lastIndex=idx;
    }

    const freeze=nodes[nodes.length-1];
    const alreadyLast=freeze===head.lastElementChild;
    if(!inOrder||!alreadyLast){
      const frag=document.createDocumentFragment();
      for(const node of nodes)frag.appendChild(node);
      head.appendChild(frag);
    }

    document.documentElement.dataset.deenTypographyLocked='1';
    return true;
  }

  // One initial pass is enough because these styles ship in the final release segment.
  // A second delayed pass covers any late parser/layout timing without a permanent observer.
  enforce();
  if(document.readyState==='loading'){
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',enforce,{once:true});
  }
  setTimeout(enforce,120);

  window.DEEN_TYPOGRAPHY_LOCK={
    version:'10.9.6',
    build:'9200metacascadefix1',
    family:'Nunito Sans',
    neonPreserved:true,
    multilingual:true,
    observer:false,
    enforce,
    check:()=>({
      locked:document.documentElement.dataset.deenTypographyLocked==='1',
      font:getComputedStyle(document.body).fontFamily,
      typography:!!document.getElementById('deen-v1089-multilingual-typography-css'),
      readability:!!document.getElementById('deen-v1090-readability-guard-css'),
      freeze:!!document.getElementById('deen-v1091-typography-freeze-css'),
      observer:false
    })
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v1093-stage-transition-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV1093StageTransition)return;
  window.__deenV1093StageTransition=true;

  const BASE_PREVIEW=typeof window.preview==='function'?window.preview.bind(window):null;
  if(!BASE_PREVIEW)return;

  let busy=false,timer=0;
  const motionOff=()=>{
    try{
      return !!state?.settings?.reducedMotion||
        state?.settings?.animation===false||
        matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    }catch(_){
      return matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    }
  };

  function sourceFor(id){
    const node=document.querySelector('#learnScreen .lp-node[data-stage="'+CSS.escape(String(id))+'"]');
    return node?.closest('.lp-stop')||document.getElementById('stop-'+id)||null;
  }

  function cleanup(){
    clearTimeout(timer);
    document.querySelectorAll('#learnScreen .lp-stop.deen-stage-transition-source')
      .forEach(x=>x.classList.remove('deen-stage-transition-source'));
    document.getElementById('learnScreen')?.classList.remove('deen-stage-transition-out');
    busy=false;
  }

  function animatePreview(id){
    if(!id)return BASE_PREVIEW(id);
    if(busy)return false;
    if(motionOff())return BASE_PREVIEW(id);

    const source=sourceFor(id);
    if(!source)return BASE_PREVIEW(id);

    const node=source.querySelector('.lp-node');
    if(node?.classList.contains('locked'))return BASE_PREVIEW(id);

    busy=true;
    source.classList.add('deen-stage-transition-source');
    document.getElementById('learnScreen')?.classList.add('deen-stage-transition-out');

    timer=setTimeout(()=>{
      try{
        BASE_PREVIEW(id);
        const sheet=document.getElementById('v5Sheet');
        if(sheet?.open){
          sheet.classList.remove('deen-stage-enter');
          void sheet.offsetWidth;
          sheet.classList.add('deen-stage-enter');
          setTimeout(()=>sheet.classList.remove('deen-stage-enter'),520);
        }
      }finally{
        setTimeout(cleanup,90);
      }
    },300);
    return true;
  }

  window.preview=animatePreview;
  try{preview=animatePreview}catch(_){}

  document.addEventListener('close',e=>{
    if(e.target?.id==='v5Sheet')cleanup();
  },true);

  window.DEEN_STAGE_TRANSITION={
    version:'10.9.3',
    build:'9204stagetransition1',
    preview:animatePreview,
    cleanup,
    snapshot:()=>({
      busy,
      source:document.querySelector('.deen-stage-transition-source')?.id||null,
      sheetAnimated:document.getElementById('v5Sheet')?.classList.contains('deen-stage-enter')||false,
      reduced:motionOff()
    })
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v1094-stage-launch-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV1094StageLaunch)return;
  window.__deenV1094StageLaunch=true;

  let lastStage=null;
  let launchCount=0;
  let lastAt=0;

  function removeCurtain(){
    document.getElementById('deenStageLaunchCurtain')?.remove();
  }

  function curtain(stageId){
    const now=Date.now();
    if(now-lastAt<120)return document.getElementById('deenStageLaunchCurtain');
    lastAt=now;
    lastStage=String(stageId||'');
    launchCount++;

    removeCurtain();

    const phone=document.getElementById('phone');
    const host=document.body||document.documentElement;
    if(!host)return null;

    const rect=phone?.getBoundingClientRect?.();
    const left=rect?Math.round(rect.left):0;
    const top=rect?Math.round(rect.top):0;
    const width=rect?Math.max(1,Math.round(rect.width)):window.innerWidth;
    const height=rect?Math.max(1,Math.round(rect.height)):window.innerHeight;

    const el=document.createElement('div');
    el.id='deenStageLaunchCurtain';
    el.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    Object.assign(el.style,{
      position:'fixed',
      left:left+'px',
      top:top+'px',
      width:width+'px',
      height:height+'px',
      overflow:'hidden',
      pointerEvents:'none',
      zIndex:'2147483646',
      background:'transparent',
      isolation:'isolate'
    });

    try{
      el.style.borderRadius=phone?getComputedStyle(phone).borderRadius:'0px';
    }catch(_){
      el.style.borderRadius='0px';
    }

    const soft=document.createElement('div');
    const main=document.createElement('div');
    const shine=document.createElement('div');

    for(const layer of [soft,main]){
      Object.assign(layer.style,{
        position:'absolute',
        left:'-12%',
        top:'-128%',
        width:'124%',
        height:'132%',
        background:'#31EFCF',
        willChange:'transform',
        transform:'translate3d(0,-18%,0)',
        backfaceVisibility:'hidden'
      });
    }

    Object.assign(soft.style,{
      opacity:'.30',
      borderRadius:'0 0 48% 52% / 0 0 12% 10%'
    });

    Object.assign(main.style,{
      borderRadius:'0 0 43% 57% / 0 0 10% 13%',
      boxShadow:'0 22px 46px rgba(49,239,207,.18)'
    });

    Object.assign(shine.style,{
      position:'absolute',
      left:'-5%',
      top:'-12px',
      width:'110%',
      height:'24px',
      borderRadius:'50%',
      background:'#31EFCF',
      opacity:'.45',
      filter:'blur(7px)'
    });
    main.appendChild(shine);

    el.append(soft,main);
    host.appendChild(el);

    const timing={duration:940,easing:'cubic-bezier(.58,.02,.22,.98)',fill:'forwards'};
    const mainAnim=main.animate([
      {transform:'translate3d(0,-18%,0)',offset:0},
      {transform:'translate3d(0,97%,0)',offset:.43},
      {transform:'translate3d(0,97%,0)',offset:.58},
      {transform:'translate3d(0,190%,0)',offset:1}
    ],timing);

    soft.animate([
      {transform:'translate3d(0,-23%,0)',offset:0},
      {transform:'translate3d(0,91%,0)',offset:.40},
      {transform:'translate3d(0,91%,0)',offset:.58},
      {transform:'translate3d(0,184%,0)',offset:1}
    ],{...timing,duration:980});

    mainAnim.finished
      .catch(()=>{})
      .finally(()=>setTimeout(removeCurtain,80));

    return el;
  }

  function revealLesson(){
    const lesson=document.getElementById('lesson');
    if(!lesson)return;
    lesson.classList.remove('deen-stage-launch-in');
    void lesson.offsetWidth;
    lesson.classList.add('deen-stage-launch-in');
    setTimeout(()=>lesson.classList.remove('deen-stage-launch-in'),620);
  }

  // Authoritative trigger: Runtime Arbiter emits this immediately before
  // every real stage start. No button selector or startStage wrapper needed.
  document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-starting',event=>{
    const stageId=event?.detail?.stageId||'';
    curtain(stageId);
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(revealLesson));
  },true);

  // Fallback for any future code path that activates lesson without emitting
  // the lifecycle event. MutationObserver only fires when lesson becomes active.
  const lesson=document.getElementById('lesson');
  if(lesson&&'MutationObserver' in window){
    let wasActive=lesson.classList.contains('active');
    new MutationObserver(()=>{
      const active=lesson.classList.contains('active');
      if(active&&!wasActive&&!document.getElementById('deenStageLaunchCurtain')){
        let sid='';
        try{sid=String(session?.stage?.id||'')}catch(_){}
        curtain(sid);
        revealLesson();
      }
      wasActive=active;
    }).observe(lesson,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
  }

  window.DEEN_STAGE_LAUNCH={
    version:'10.9.8',
    build:'9209lessonstartingwipe1',
    start:(stageId)=>curtain(stageId),
    curtain,
    snapshot:()=>({
      lastStage,
      launchCount,
      curtain:!!document.getElementById('deenStageLaunchCurtain'),
      lifecycle:true,
      fallbackObserver:!!lesson
    })
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v10910-badge-surface-audit-js">
(()=>{
  let lessonBefore=null;
  let sectionBefore=null;
  let lastResultBadges=[];

  function achievements(){
    try{return Array.isArray(state?.achievements)?[...state.achievements]:[]}
    catch(_){return[]}
  }
  function snapshot(){return new Set(achievements())}
  function diff(before){
    const b=before||new Set();
    return achievements().filter(id=>!b.has(id)&&window.DEEN_BADGE_ART?.known?.(id));
  }
  function clearResultArt(){
    const medal=document.getElementById('resultMedal');
    if(!medal)return;
    medal.classList.remove('deen-result-badge-art','deen-badge-art');
    delete medal.dataset.badgeId;
    medal.style.removeProperty('background-image');
    medal.style.removeProperty('background-position');
    medal.style.removeProperty('background-size');
    medal.style.removeProperty('background-repeat');
    medal.removeAttribute('role');
    medal.removeAttribute('aria-label');
  }
  function renderResultBadge(ids){
    const id=(ids||[])[0];
    if(!id||!window.DEEN_BADGE_ART?.known?.(id))return false;
    const medal=document.getElementById('resultMedal');
    if(!medal)return false;
    medal.classList.add('deen-result-badge-art');
    medal.dataset.badgeId=id;
    medal.setAttribute('role','img');
    let label=id;
    try{label=badges().find(b=>b.id===id)?.name||id}catch(_){}
    medal.setAttribute('aria-label','Yeni rozet · '+label);
    window.DEEN_BADGE_ART.apply(medal,id,{size:118});
    lastResultBadges=[...ids];
    return true;
  }

  document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-result-before',()=>{
    lessonBefore=snapshot();
    lastResultBadges=[];
    clearResultArt();
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('deen:lesson-result',()=>{
    const ids=diff(lessonBefore);
    lessonBefore=null;
    if(ids.length)requestAnimationFrame(()=>renderResultBadge(ids));
  },true);

  document.addEventListener('deen:section-result-before',()=>{
    sectionBefore=snapshot();
    lastResultBadges=[];
    clearResultArt();
  },true);
  document.addEventListener('deen:section-result',()=>{
    const ids=diff(sectionBefore);
    sectionBefore=null;
    if(ids.length)requestAnimationFrame(()=>renderResultBadge(ids));
  },true);

  function audit(){
    const registry=window.DEEN_BADGE_ART;
    let defined=[];
    try{defined=badges().map(b=>String(b.id))}catch(_){}
    const atlas=registry?.ids?[...registry.ids]:[];
    const missingAchievementArt=defined.filter(id=>!registry?.known?.(id));
    const orphanAtlas=atlas.filter(id=>!defined.includes(id));
    const savedUnknown=achievements().filter(id=>!registry?.known?.(id));
    let unitTitles=[];
    try{unitTitles=Array.isArray(UNIT_TITLES)?[...UNIT_TITLES]:[]}catch(_){}
    const openPopup=document.querySelector('.gf-reward-modal[open],.gf-reward-modal');
    const popupArt=openPopup?.querySelector('.deen-reward-badge-art[data-badge-id]')||null;
    const popupMissing=openPopup?.querySelector('.deen-reward-badge-missing')||null;
    const resultArt=document.querySelector('#resultMedal.deen-result-badge-art[data-badge-id]');
    const profile=registry?.check?.()||null;
    const issues=[];
    if(!registry)issues.push('achievement_registry_missing');
    if(missingAchievementArt.length)issues.push('achievement_art_missing');
    if(savedUnknown.length)issues.push('saved_achievement_unknown');
    if(popupMissing)issues.push('open_reward_badge_missing_art');
    return{
      version:'10.9.11',
      build:'9212badgecleanup1',
      achievement:{
        defined,
        atlas,
        missingAchievementArt,
        orphanAtlas,
        savedUnknown,
        profile,
        popup:popupArt?.dataset.badgeId||null,
        result:resultArt?.dataset.badgeId||null,
        lastResultBadges:[...lastResultBadges]
      },
      unitTitles:{
        expected:unitTitles.length,
        values:[...unitTitles],
        collectible:false,
        note:'Unit completion labels are progression titles, not collectible badges. They intentionally have no badge artwork.'
      },
      issues,
      pass:issues.length===0
    };
  }

  window.DEEN_BADGE_AUDIT={version:'10.9.11',build:'9212badgecleanup1',run:audit,snapshot:audit};
  document.documentElement.dataset.deenBadgeSurfaces='v10910';
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v10913-admin-panel-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10913AdminPanel)return;
  window.__deenV10913AdminPanel=true;

  const VERSION='10.9.15';
  const BUILD='9216adminbadges1';
  const SECTION_MAX={1:5,2:10,3:15};

  function clone(v){try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v))}catch(_){return v}}
  function retireLegacyAdmin(){
    try{document.getElementById('deenAdminOverlay')?.remove()}catch(_){}
    try{document.getElementById('deenAdminTrigger')?.remove()}catch(_){}
  }
  function units(){try{return Array.from({length:15},(_,i)=>uid(i+1))}catch(_){return Array.from({length:15},(_,i)=>'U'+String(i+1).padStart(2,'0'))}}
  function stageList(){try{return Array.isArray(STAGES)?STAGES:[]}catch(_){return[]}}
  function finalState(n){
    const key=n===1?'sectionFinal':n===2?'section2Final':'section3Final';
    if(!state[key]||typeof state[key]!=='object'){
      state[key]={attempts:0,bestScore:0,completed:false,rank:null,lastKnowledge:0,lastRetention:0};
    }
    return state[key];
  }
  function refreshRuntime(){
    try{window.DEEN_FIVE_STAGE?.sync?.()}catch(_){}
    try{renderPaths?.()}catch(_){}
    try{updateUI?.()}catch(_){}
    try{returnCard?.()}catch(_){}
    try{window.DEEN_PROFILE_COMPANION_SYNC?.apply?.()}catch(_){}
    try{window.DEEN_BADGE_ART?.hydrate?.(document)}catch(_){}
  }
  function persist(message){
    try{saveState?.()}catch(_){}
    refreshRuntime();
    render();
    notify(message);
  }
  function notify(message){
    const el=document.getElementById('deenAdminMessage');
    if(!el)return;
    el.textContent=message||'Tamamlandı.';
    el.classList.add('show');
    clearTimeout(notify.t);
    notify.t=setTimeout(()=>el.classList.remove('show'),2600);
  }
  function ensureArrays(){
    if(!Array.isArray(state.completed))state.completed=[];
    if(!state.stageScores||typeof state.stageScores!=='object')state.stageScores={};
    if(!Array.isArray(state.celebratedUnits))state.celebratedUnits=[];
    if(!Array.isArray(state.celebratedSections))state.celebratedSections=[];
    if(!Array.isArray(state.achievements))state.achievements=[];
    if(!Array.isArray(state.history))state.history=[];
    state.profile=state.profile&&typeof state.profile==='object'?state.profile:{};
    state.streakMeta=state.streakMeta&&typeof state.streakMeta==='object'?state.streakMeta:{};
  }
  function completeStages(filter){
    ensureArrays();
    const list=stageList().filter(filter);
    const done=new Set(state.completed);
    for(const s of list){
      done.add(s.id);
      state.stageScores[s.id]=100;
    }
    state.completed=[...done];
    return list.length;
  }
  function completeThrough(maxUnit){
    const count=completeStages(s=>Number(String(s.unit||'').slice(1))<=maxUnit);
    const doneUnits=units().slice(0,maxUnit);
    state.celebratedUnits=[...new Set([...(state.celebratedUnits||[]),...doneUnits])];
    return count;
  }
  function passFinal(n){
    const f=finalState(n);
    f.attempts=Math.max(1,Number(f.attempts)||0);
    f.bestScore=100;
    f.completed=true;
    f.rank='ALTIN USTALIK';
    f.lastKnowledge=100;
    f.lastRetention=100;
    state.celebratedSections=[...new Set([...(state.celebratedSections||[]),n])];
  }
  function passSection(n){
    ensureArrays();
    const max=SECTION_MAX[n]||5;
    const count=completeThrough(max);
    for(let i=1;i<=n;i++)passFinal(i);
    persist('Kısım '+n+' tamamlandı · '+count+' teknik aşama %100.');
  }
  function clearStageRuntime(ids){
    const set=new Set(ids);
    if(state.stageLessonMeta&&typeof state.stageLessonMeta==='object'){
      for(const id of set)delete state.stageLessonMeta[id];
    }
    if(state.stageAttemptCounts&&typeof state.stageAttemptCounts==='object'){
      for(const id of set)delete state.stageAttemptCounts[id];
    }
    if(state.stageAttempts&&typeof state.stageAttempts==='object'){
      for(const key of Object.keys(state.stageAttempts)){
        if([...set].some(id=>key===id||key.endsWith(':'+id)))delete state.stageAttempts[key];
      }
    }
  }
  function resetSection(n,{all=false}={}){
    ensureArrays();
    n=Math.max(1,Math.min(3,Number(n)||1));
    const minUnit=all?1:(n===1?1:n===2?6:11);
    const maxUnit=15;
    const affectedStages=stageList().filter(s=>{
      const u=Number(String(s.unit||'').slice(1));
      return u>=minUnit&&u<=maxUnit;
    });
    const affectedIds=new Set(affectedStages.map(s=>s.id));
    const affectedUnits=new Set(units().filter(u=>Number(u.slice(1))>=minUnit));

    state.completed=state.completed.filter(id=>!affectedIds.has(id));
    for(const id of affectedIds)delete state.stageScores[id];
    clearStageRuntime([...affectedIds]);

    state.celebratedUnits=(state.celebratedUnits||[]).filter(u=>!affectedUnits.has(u));
    state.history=(state.history||[]).filter(h=>{
      const u=Number(String(h?.unit||'').slice(1));
      return !Number.isFinite(u)||u<minUnit;
    });

    for(let i=n;i<=3;i++){
      const f=finalState(i);
      Object.assign(f,{attempts:0,bestScore:0,completed:false,rank:null,lastKnowledge:0,lastRetention:0});
    }
    state.celebratedSections=(state.celebratedSections||[]).filter(x=>Number(x)<n);

    persist(all
      ? 'Tüm bölüm ilerlemesi sıfırlandı.'
      : 'Kısım '+n+' ve sonraki kısımlar sıfırlandı.');
  }
  function passAll(){
    ensureArrays();
    const count=completeThrough(15);
    passFinal(1);passFinal(2);passFinal(3);
    persist('Tüm içerik tamamlandı · '+count+' teknik aşama %100 · 3 final geçti.');
  }
  function passSelectedStage(){
    const id=document.getElementById('deenAdminStage')?.value;
    const s=stageList().find(x=>x.id===id);
    if(!s)return notify('Aşama bulunamadı.');
    completeStages(x=>x.id===id);
    persist(id+' %100 tamamlandı.');
  }
  function resetSelectedStage(){
    const id=document.getElementById('deenAdminStage')?.value;
    if(!id)return;
    ensureArrays();
    state.completed=state.completed.filter(x=>x!==id);
    delete state.stageScores[id];
    persist(id+' geri alındı.');
  }
  function startSelectedStage(){
    const id=document.getElementById('deenAdminStage')?.value;
    if(!id)return;
    close();
    const prev=previewMode;
    try{
      previewMode=true;
      if(typeof preview==='function')preview(id);
      else if(typeof startStage==='function')startStage(id);
    }finally{
      setTimeout(()=>{try{previewMode=prev}catch(_){}},0);
    }
  }
  function passSelectedUnit(){
    const u=document.getElementById('deenAdminUnit')?.value;
    if(!u)return;
    const count=completeStages(s=>s.unit===u);
    state.celebratedUnits=[...new Set([...(state.celebratedUnits||[]),u])];
    persist(u+' tamamlandı · '+count+' aşama %100.');
  }
  function resetSelectedUnit(){
    const u=document.getElementById('deenAdminUnit')?.value;
    if(!u)return;
    ensureArrays();
    const ids=new Set(stageList().filter(s=>s.unit===u).map(s=>s.id));
    state.completed=state.completed.filter(id=>!ids.has(id));
    ids.forEach(id=>delete state.stageScores[id]);
    state.celebratedUnits=state.celebratedUnits.filter(x=>x!==u);
    persist(u+' ilerlemesi geri alındı.');
  }
  function passOneFinal(n){
    ensureArrays();passFinal(n);persist('Kısım '+n+' finali Altın Ustalık olarak geçti.');
  }
  function resetFinal(n){
    const f=finalState(n);
    Object.assign(f,{attempts:0,bestScore:0,completed:false,rank:null,lastKnowledge:0,lastRetention:0});
    state.celebratedSections=(state.celebratedSections||[]).filter(x=>x!==n);
    persist('Kısım '+n+' finali sıfırlandı.');
  }
  function unlockBadges(){
    ensureArrays();
    let defs=[];
    try{defs=badges().map(b=>({id:b.id,name:b.name,desc:b.desc}))}catch(_){
      defs=(window.DEEN_BADGE_ART?.ids||[]).map(id=>({id,name:id,desc:'Başarı rozeti'}));
    }
    const ids=defs.map(b=>b.id);
    const before=new Set(state.achievements||[]);
    const newly=defs.filter(b=>!before.has(b.id));
    state.achievements=[...new Set([...(state.achievements||[]),...ids])];
    try{saveState?.()}catch(_){}
    refreshRuntime();
    close();

    // Admin action is also a visual QA path: always replay the real reward UI,
    // even if these IDs were persisted by an earlier broken admin build.
    setTimeout(()=>{
      try{
        for(const b of defs){
          celebrate({
            type:'achievement',
            id:b.id,
            name:b.name,
            title:'Yeni rozet · '+b.name,
            detail:b.desc,
            adminPreview:true
          });
        }
        runCelebrations?.();
      }catch(err){
        console.error('DEEN admin badge showcase',err);
      }
    },90);

    setTimeout(()=>{
      try{window.DEEN_BADGE_ART?.hydrate?.(document)}catch(_){}
      try{if(document.querySelector('.screen.active')?.id==='profileScreen')profileView?.()}catch(_){}
    },140);

    return{unlocked:ids.length,newly:newly.map(b=>b.id),replayed:defs.length};
  }
  function clearBadges(){
    state.achievements=[];
    try{saveState?.()}catch(_){}
    refreshRuntime();
    render();
    notify('Admin tarafından açılan achievement kayıtları temizlendi.');
  }
  function setEnergy(maxOnly=false){
    ensureArrays();
    state.energy=30;
    state.lastEnergyAt=Date.now();
    if(!maxOnly)state.premium=true;
    persist(maxOnly?'Enerji 30/30 yapıldı.':'Sınırsız enerji açıldı (admin premium flag).');
  }
  function normalEnergy(){
    state.premium=false;
    state.energy=Math.min(30,Math.max(0,Number(state.energy)||30));
    persist('Normal enerji sistemi geri açıldı.');
  }
  function addCurrency(value){
    state.currency=Math.max(0,Number(value)||0);
    persist('Pırıltı: '+state.currency);
  }
  function addXP(value){
    state.xp=Math.max(0,Number(value)||0);
    persist('XP: '+state.xp);
  }
  function setStreak(value){
    ensureArrays();
    const n=Math.max(0,Number(value)||0);
    state.streak=n;
    state.streakMeta.currentStreak=n;
    state.streakMeta.longestStreak=Math.max(n,Number(state.streakMeta.longestStreak)||0);
    persist('Seri: '+n+' gün.');
  }
  function previewUnlock(on){
    try{previewMode=!!on}catch(_){}
    render();
    notify(on?'Tüm kilitler geçici açıldı.':'Normal kilit sistemi geri açıldı.');
  }
  function clearProgress(){
    if(!confirm('Tüm ders/final ilerlemesi ve rozetler sıfırlansın mı? Profil ve onboarding korunacak.'))return;
    ensureArrays();
    state.completed=[];
    state.stageScores={};
    state.celebratedUnits=[];
    state.celebratedSections=[];
    state.achievements=[];
    state.history=[];
    if(state.stageAttempts&&typeof state.stageAttempts==='object')state.stageAttempts={};
    for(const n of [1,2,3])Object.assign(finalState(n),{attempts:0,bestScore:0,completed:false,rank:null,lastKnowledge:0,lastRetention:0});
    try{window.DEEN_FIVE_STAGE?.sync?.()}catch(_){}
    persist('İlerleme sıfırlandı; profil korundu.');
  }
  function fullReset(){
    if(!confirm('TÜM yerel oyun verisi sıfırlansın mı? Profil, onboarding, ilerleme ve ayarlar silinir.'))return;
    try{storageRemove?.('deen_v4_1_state')}catch(_){}
    try{storageRemove?.('deen_v57_last_good_state')}catch(_){}
    try{
      const base=clone(DEFAULT_STATE);
      state=typeof migrate==='function'?migrate(base,false):base;
      saveState?.();
    }catch(_){}
    close();
    try{window.DEEN_PREMIUM_ONBOARDING?.render?.(0)}catch(_){location.reload()}
  }
  function openEntry(reset=false){
    close();
    if(reset){
      try{state.onboarded=false;state.onboardingVersion=0;saveState?.()}catch(_){}
    }
    try{window.DEEN_PREMIUM_ONBOARDING?.render?.(0)}
    catch(_){try{onboarding?.(0)}catch(__){}}
  }
  function go(screen){
    close();
    try{navigate?.(screen)}catch(_){}
  }
  function audit(){
    let result={};
    try{
      result={
        badge:window.DEEN_BADGE_AUDIT?.run?.()||null,
        runtime:window.DEEN_RUNTIME_ARBITER?.snapshot?.()||window.DEEN_RUNTIME_REGRESSION?.snapshot?.()||null,
        admin:snapshot()
      };
    }catch(e){result={error:String(e?.message||e)}}
    const box=document.getElementById('deenAdminJson');
    if(box){box.textContent=JSON.stringify(result,null,2);box.classList.add('show')}
  }
  async function copyState(){
    try{
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(state,null,2));
      notify('State JSON panoya kopyalandı.');
    }catch(_){notify('Panoya kopyalama desteklenmedi.')}
  }
  function snapshot(){
    ensureArrays();
    return{
      version:VERSION,build:BUILD,
      stages:{completed:state.completed.length,total:stageList().length},
      finals:[1,2,3].map(n=>!!finalState(n).completed),
      achievements:state.achievements.length,
      xp:Number(state.xp)||0,
      currency:Number(state.currency)||0,
      energy:state.premium?'∞':Number(state.energy)||0,
      streak:Number(state.streak)||0,
      preview:!!previewMode,
      onboarded:!!state.onboarded
    };
  }

  function optionsStage(){
    return stageList().map(s=>'<option value="'+s.id+'">'+s.id+' · '+String(s.title||s.kind||'Aşama')+'</option>').join('');
  }
  function optionsUnit(){
    const names=Array.isArray(window.U)?window.U:(typeof U!=='undefined'?U:[]);
    return units().map((u,i)=>'<option value="'+u+'">'+u+' · '+String(names[i]||('Ünite '+(i+1)))+'</option>').join('');
  }
  function ensureDialog(){
    let d=document.getElementById('deenAdminPanelV2');
    if(d)return d;
    d=document.createElement('dialog');
    d.id='deenAdminPanelV2';
    d.innerHTML='<div class="deen-admin-shell"><div class="deen-admin-head"><div><b>DEEN ADMIN</b><small>P · DEVELOPER CONTROL CENTER</small></div><button class="deen-admin-close" type="button" aria-label="Admin panelini kapat">×</button></div><div class="deen-admin-body" id="deenAdminBody"></div></div>';
    document.body.appendChild(d);
    d.querySelector('.deen-admin-close').onclick=close;
    d.addEventListener('cancel',e=>{e.preventDefault();close()});
    d.addEventListener('click',event=>{
      const b=event.target.closest('[data-admin]');
      if(!b)return;
      const a=b.dataset.admin;
      const arg=b.dataset.arg;
      const fn=actions[a];
      if(fn)fn(arg);
    });
    return d;
  }
  function render(){
    const d=ensureDialog(),body=d.querySelector('#deenAdminBody');
    const s=snapshot();
    body.innerHTML=
      '<div id="deenAdminMessage"></div>'+
      '<div class="deen-admin-status">'+
        '<div class="deen-admin-stat"><strong>'+s.stages.completed+'/'+s.stages.total+'</strong><span>AŞAMA</span></div>'+
        '<div class="deen-admin-stat"><strong>'+s.achievements+'/10</strong><span>ROZET</span></div>'+
        '<div class="deen-admin-stat"><strong>'+s.xp+'</strong><span>XP</span></div>'+
        '<div class="deen-admin-stat"><strong>'+s.energy+'</strong><span>ENERJİ</span></div>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<section class="deen-admin-section"><h3>HIZLI İLERLEME</h3><div class="deen-admin-grid">'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn primary" data-admin="passAll">TÜM BÖLÜMLERİ GEÇ</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn warn" data-admin="resetAllSections">TÜM BÖLÜMLERİ SIFIRLA</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn" data-admin="passSection" data-arg="1">KISIM 1’İ GEÇ</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn warn" data-admin="resetSection" data-arg="1">KISIM 1’İ SIFIRLA</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn" data-admin="passSection" data-arg="2">KISIM 2’Yİ GEÇ</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn warn" data-admin="resetSection" data-arg="2">KISIM 2’Yİ SIFIRLA</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn" data-admin="passSection" data-arg="3">KISIM 3’Ü GEÇ</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn warn" data-admin="resetSection" data-arg="3">KISIM 3’Ü SIFIRLA</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn gold" data-admin="unlockBadges">TÜM ROZETLERİ AÇ</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn '+(s.preview?'gold':'dark')+'" data-admin="previewUnlock" data-arg="'+(s.preview?'0':'1')+'">'+(s.preview?'KİLİTLERİ NORMALLEŞTİR':'TÜM KİLİTLERİ AÇ')+'</button>'+
      '</div></section>'+
      '<section class="deen-admin-section"><h3>TEK AŞAMA KONTROLÜ</h3>'+
        '<select class="deen-admin-select" id="deenAdminStage">'+optionsStage()+'</select><div class="deen-admin-grid">'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn primary" data-admin="passStage">SEÇİLİ AŞAMAYI GEÇ</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn" data-admin="startStage">AŞAMAYI AÇ / BAŞLAT</button>'+

      '</div></section>'+

        '<button class="deen-admin-btn" data-admin="entry">GİRİŞ EKRANINA DÖN</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn" data-admin="resetOnboarding">ONBOARDING’İ BAŞTAN AÇ</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn" data-admin="profile">PROFİLE GİT</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn" data-admin="goLearn">ÖĞREN EKRANINA GİT</button>'+
      '</div><p class="deen-admin-note">“Giriş ekranına dön” mevcut progress’i silmez. “Onboarding’i baştan aç” sadece onboarding tamamlandı bayrağını sıfırlar.</p></section>'+
      '<section class="deen-admin-section"><h3>QA / STATE</h3><div class="deen-admin-grid">'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn" data-admin="audit">ROZET + RUNTIME AUDIT</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn" data-admin="copyState">STATE JSON KOPYALA</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn warn" data-admin="clearProgress">İLERLEMEYİ SIFIRLA</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-admin-btn warn" data-admin="fullReset">TAM SIFIRLAMA</button>'+
      '</div><pre class="deen-admin-json" id="deenAdminJson"></pre></section>';
    return d;
  }
  function open(){
    retireLegacyAdmin();
    try{document.getElementById('v5Sheet')?.open&&document.getElementById('v5Sheet').close()}catch(_){}
    const d=render();
    if(!d.open)d.showModal();
  }
  function close(){
    const d=document.getElementById('deenAdminPanelV2');
    if(d?.open)d.close();
  }
  function toggle(){document.getElementById('deenAdminPanelV2')?.open?close():open()}

  const actions={
    passAll,
    unlockBadges,
    clearBadges,
    passSection:n=>passSection(Number(n)),
    resetSection:n=>resetSection(Number(n)),
    resetAllSections:()=>resetSection(1,{all:true}),
    previewUnlock:v=>previewUnlock(v==='1'),
    passStage:passSelectedStage,
    resetStage:resetSelectedStage,
    startStage:startSelectedStage,
    passUnit:passSelectedUnit,
    resetUnit:resetSelectedUnit,
    passFinal:n=>passOneFinal(Number(n)),
    resetFinal:n=>resetFinal(Number(n)),
    unlimitedEnergy:()=>setEnergy(false),
    maxEnergy:()=>setEnergy(true),
    normalEnergy,
    currencyMax:()=>addCurrency(999999),
    xp5000:()=>addXP(5000),
    xp50000:()=>addXP(50000),
    streak100:()=>setStreak(100),
    entry:()=>openEntry(false),
    resetOnboarding:()=>openEntry(true),
    profile:()=>go('profileScreen'),
    goLearn:()=>go('learnScreen'),
    audit,
    copyState,
    clearProgress,
    fullReset
  };

  retireLegacyAdmin();
  setTimeout(retireLegacyAdmin,150);
  setTimeout(retireLegacyAdmin,950);

  document.addEventListener('keydown',event=>{
    if(event.defaultPrevented||event.ctrlKey||event.metaKey||event.altKey)return;
    const t=event.target;
    if(t&&(['INPUT','TEXTAREA','SELECT'].includes(t.tagName)||t.isContentEditable))return;
    if(String(event.key||'').toLowerCase()!=='p')return;
    event.preventDefault();
    toggle();
  },true);

  window.DEEN_ADMIN={
    authoritative:true,version:VERSION,build:BUILD,open,close,toggle,render,snapshot,
    passAll,passSection,resetSection,passFinal:passOneFinal,
    passStage:id=>{const s=stageList().find(x=>x.id===id);if(!s)return false;completeStages(x=>x.id===id);persist(id+' tamamlandı.');return true},
    passUnit:u=>{const count=completeStages(s=>s.unit===u);persist(u+' · '+count+' aşama tamamlandı.');return count},
    unlockBadges,
    entry:()=>openEntry(false),
    onboarding:()=>openEntry(true),
    audit
  };
  document.documentElement.dataset.deenAdmin='v10915';
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v10916-quran-hub-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10916QuranHub)return;
  window.__deenV10916QuranHub=true;

  const VERSION='10.9.16';
  const BUILD='9217quranhub1';
  const SURAHS=[
    {id:'fatiha',name:'Fâtiha',ayahs:7,order:1},
    {id:'ikhlas',name:'İhlâs',ayahs:4,order:112},
    {id:'falaq',name:'Felak',ayahs:5,order:113},
    {id:'nas',name:'Nâs',ayahs:6,order:114},
    {id:'kafirun',name:'Kâfirûn',ayahs:6,order:109},
    {id:'nasr',name:'Nasr',ayahs:3,order:110},
    {id:'masad',name:'Tebbet',ayahs:5,order:111},
    {id:'fil',name:'Fîl',ayahs:5,order:105},
    {id:'quraysh',name:'Kureyş',ayahs:4,order:106},
    {id:'maun',name:'Mâûn',ayahs:7,order:107},
    {id:'sharh',name:'İnşirah',ayahs:8,order:94},
    {id:'tin',name:'Tîn',ayahs:8,order:95},
    {id:'bayyina',name:'Beyyine',ayahs:8,order:98},
    {id:'zilzal',name:'Zilzâl',ayahs:8,order:99},
    {id:'kawthar',name:'Kevser',ayahs:3,order:108},
    {id:'asr',name:'Asr',ayahs:3,order:103}
  ];

  function ensure(){
    state.quranJourney=state.quranJourney&&typeof state.quranJourney==='object'?state.quranJourney:{};
    const q=state.quranJourney;
    if(!Array.isArray(q.startedSurahs))q.startedSurahs=[];
    if(!Array.isArray(q.memorizedSurahs))q.memorizedSurahs=[];
    if(!Array.isArray(q.reviewQueue))q.reviewQueue=[];
    if(!SURAHS.some(s=>s.id===q.currentSurah))q.currentSurah='fatiha';
    q.verseLearned=Math.max(0,Number(q.verseLearned)||0);
    q.version=1;
    return q;
  }
  function current(){
    const q=ensure();
    return SURAHS.find(s=>s.id===q.currentSurah)||SURAHS[0];
  }
  function save(){
    try{saveState?.()}catch(_){}
  }
  function nav(){
    const b=document.querySelector('.bottomnav .navbtn[data-screen="leagueScreen"]');
    if(!b)return false;
    const label=b.querySelector('span');
    if(label)label.textContent='Kur’an';
    b.setAttribute('aria-label','Kur’an öğrenme merkezi');
    const svg=b.querySelector('svg');
    if(svg)svg.innerHTML='<path d="M4.5 5.5c2.6-.7 5-.2 7.5 1.5v11.5c-2.5-1.7-4.9-2.2-7.5-1.5z"/><path d="M19.5 5.5c-2.6-.7-5-.2-7.5 1.5v11.5c2.5-1.7 4.9-2.2 7.5-1.5z"/><path d="M12 7v11.5"/>';
    return true;
  }
  function render(){
    nav();
    const root=document.getElementById('leagueScreenBody');
    if(!root)return false;
    const q=ensure(),cur=current();
    const started=q.startedSurahs.length;
    const memorized=q.memorizedSurahs.length;
    const due=q.reviewQueue.length;
    const progress=Math.min(100,Math.round((memorized/SURAHS.length)*100));
    root.innerHTML=
      '<div class="deen-quran-shell">'+
        '<div class="deen-quran-kicker">KUR’AN ÖĞRENME MERKEZİ</div>'+
        '<h1 class="deen-quran-title">Kur’an Yolculuğun</h1>'+
        '<p class="deen-quran-sub">Sureleri adım adım öğren, ezberini güçlendir ve düzenli tekrarlarla hatırla.</p>'+
        '<section class="deen-quran-hero">'+
          '<div class="mini">'+(started?'DEVAM ET':'İLK ADIM')+'</div>'+
          '<h2>'+cur.name+' Suresi</h2>'+
          '<p>'+cur.ayahs+' ayet · Dinle → parçala → anla → hatırla → ezberden tamamla</p>'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-progress"><i style="width:'+progress+'%"></i></div>'+
          '<button class="deen-quran-primary" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.openSurah(\''+cur.id+'\')">'+(started?'ÇALIŞMAYA DEVAM ET':'SURE ÖĞRENMEYE BAŞLA')+'</button>'+
        '</section>'+
        '<div class="deen-quran-stats">'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-stat"><strong>'+started+'</strong><span>BAŞLANAN SURE</span></div>'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-stat"><strong>'+memorized+'</strong><span>EZBERLENEN</span></div>'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-stat"><strong>'+due+'</strong><span>TEKRAR</span></div>'+
        '</div>'+
        '<h2 class="deen-quran-section-title">Nasıl çalışmak istersin?</h2>'+
        '<div class="deen-quran-grid">'+
          '<button class="deen-quran-card" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.surahList()"><span class="ico">☰</span><b>Sure Öğren</b><small>Kısa sureleri adım adım öğren.</small></button>'+
          '<button class="deen-quran-card" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.memorize()"><span class="ico">◌</span><b>Ezber</b><small>Dinle, gizle, sırala ve hatırla.</small></button>'+
          '<button class="deen-quran-card" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.dailyVerse()"><span class="ico">✦</span><b>Bugünün Ayeti</b><small>Bir ayeti anlamıyla birlikte çalış.</small></button>'+
          '<button class="deen-quran-card" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.review()"><span class="ico">↻</span><b>Kur’an Tekrarı</b><small>Öğrendiğin sure ve ayetleri pekiştir.</small></button>'+
        '</div>'+
        '<p class="deen-quran-note">Kur’an metni, meal ve ses içerikleri doğrulanmış kaynak bağlantısı tamamlanmadan yayınlanmaz.</p>'+
      '</div>';
    document.documentElement.dataset.deenQuranHub='v10916';
    return true;
  }
  function surahList(){
    ensure();
    sheet('Sure Öğren',
      '<p class="v5-muted">İlk öğrenme paketi kısa surelerle başlayacak.</p>'+
      '<div class="deen-quran-list">'+SURAHS.map(s=>
        '<button type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.openSurah(\''+s.id+'\')">'+
          '<span><b>'+s.name+' Suresi</b><small>'+s.order+'. sure · '+s.ayahs+' ayet</small></span>'+
          '<em>→</em>'+
        '</button>'
      ).join('')+'</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Ayet metni, meal ve kıraat sesleri içerik doğrulama hattından geçtikçe bu öğrenme akışına bağlanacak.</div>'
    );
  }
  function openSurah(id){
    const s=SURAHS.find(x=>x.id===id)||SURAHS[0],q=ensure();
    q.currentSurah=s.id;
    if(!q.startedSurahs.includes(s.id))q.startedSurahs.push(s.id);
    save();
    render();
    sheet(s.name+' Suresi',
      '<div class="v5-kicker">ÖĞRENME AKIŞI · '+s.ayahs+' AYET</div>'+
      '<p class="v5-muted">Bu sureyi tek seferde ezberlemek yerine küçük adımlarla ilerleyeceğiz.</p>'+
      '<div class="deen-quran-flow">'+
        step('1','Dinle & takip et','Doğrulanmış kıraat kaydı eşliğinde metni takip et.')+
        step('2','Parçalara böl','Ayeti kısa, hatırlanabilir parçalara ayır.')+
        step('3','Anlamını öğren','Doğrulanmış meal ve kısa bağlam notuyla anlamı kur.')+
        step('4','Hatırla','Kelimeler ve parçalar aşamalı olarak gizlenir.')+
        step('5','Ezberden tamamla','Başlangıç ipucundan sonra devamını zihninden getir.')+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-quran-pending">İçerik paketi henüz doğrulama aşamasında. Öğrenme motoru hazır; ayet/meal/ses bağlandığında bu adımlar aktif olacak.</div>'
    );
  }
  function step(n,title,desc){
    return '<div class="deen-quran-step"><i>'+n+'</i><span><b>'+title+'</b><small>'+desc+'</small></span></div>';
  }
  function memorize(){
    sheet('Ezber Modu',
      '<p class="v5-muted">Ezber çalışması tek bir yönteme bağlı kalmayacak.</p>'+
      '<div class="deen-quran-flow">'+
        step('1','Dinle & takip et','Kıraat ilerlerken metni takip et.')+
        step('2','Gizleyerek oku','Kelimeler aşamalı kaybolur; sen devam edersin.')+
        step('3','Sırala','Ayet parçalarını doğru sıraya getir.')+
        step('4','Hatırlama testi','Yalnız başlangıç ipucu verilir; devamını hatırlarsın.')+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Telaffuz/kıraat için otomatik “doğru-yanlış” hükmü bu ilk sürümde verilmeyecek. İlk aşama dinleme ve self-check odaklı olacak.</div>'
    );
  }
  function dailyVerse(){
    sheet('Bugünün Ayeti',
      '<div class="v5-kicker">GÜNLÜK AYET ÇALIŞMASI</div>'+
      '<h2 style="margin:8px 0 6px">Ayet havuzu hazırlanıyor</h2>'+
      '<p class="v5-muted">Burada Arapça ayet, sure/ayet numarası, doğrulanmış Türkçe meal ve kısa öğrenme notu birlikte gösterilecek.</p>'+
      '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Doğrulanmış kaynak bağlanmadan rastgele ayet veya meal yayınlamıyoruz.</div>'
    );
  }
  function review(){
    const q=ensure();
    sheet('Kur’an Tekrarı',
      '<div class="v5-kicker">'+q.reviewQueue.length+' TEKRAR BEKLİYOR</div>'+
      '<h2 style="margin:8px 0 6px">'+(q.reviewQueue.length?'Bugünkü ezber tekrarların':'Şimdilik tekrar yok')+'</h2>'+
      '<p class="v5-muted">'+(q.reviewQueue.length?'Öğrendiğin sure ve ayetler unutma aralığına göre burada sıralanacak.':'İlk sure çalışmalarını tamamladıkça tekrar planın burada oluşacak.')+'</p>'+
      '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Kur’an tekrarları genel “Tekrar” sekmesinden ayrı tutulur; burada yalnız sure/ayet ezberi yönetilir.</div>'
    );
  }

  // Retire user-facing league mechanics while preserving legacy state compatibility.
  window.rollWeek=function quranEraWeekRoll(){
    try{
      const w=week();
      if(state.league&&state.league.week!==w){
        state.league.week=w;
        state.league.xp=0;
      }
    }catch(_){}
  };

  window.leagueView=render;
  try{leagueView=render}catch(_){}

  const baseProfile=window.profileView;
  if(typeof baseProfile==='function'&&!baseProfile.__deenQuranWrapped){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const out=baseProfile.apply(this,arguments);
      try{
        const q=ensure();
        const metrics=document.querySelectorAll('#profileScreenBody .v5-profilemetrics > div');
        const last=metrics[metrics.length-1];
        if(last){
          const strong=last.querySelector('strong');
          const small=last.querySelector('small');
          if(strong)strong.textContent=String(q.memorizedSurahs.length);
          if(small)small.textContent='Ezberlenen sure';
        }
      }catch(_){}
      return out;
    };
    wrapped.__deenQuranWrapped=true;
    window.profileView=wrapped;
    try{profileView=wrapped}catch(_){}
  }

  window.DEEN_QURAN={
    version:VERSION,
    build:BUILD,
    surahs:SURAHS.map(x=>({...x})),
    render,
    surahList,
    openSurah,
    memorize,
    dailyVerse,
    review,
    state:()=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ensure())),
    check:()=>({
      nav:document.querySelector('.bottomnav .navbtn[data-screen="leagueScreen"] span')?.textContent||null,
      body:!!document.querySelector('#leagueScreenBody .deen-quran-shell'),
      leagueVisible:/\bLig\b/i.test(document.getElementById('leagueScreen')?.innerText||''),
      current:current().id
    })
  };

  nav();
  if(document.querySelector('.screen.active')?.id==='leagueScreen')render();
  document.addEventListener('deen:navigated',e=>{
    nav();
    if(document.querySelector('.screen.active')?.id==='leagueScreen')render();
  });
  setTimeout(nav,80);
  setTimeout(nav,500);
})();
</script>


<!-- DEEN v10.9.17 — Quran persistent data architecture v2 -->
<script id="deen-v10917-quran-data-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10917QuranData)return;
  window.__deenV10917QuranData=true;

  const SCHEMA_VERSION=2;
  const SURAH_IDS=['fatiha','ikhlas','falaq','nas','kafirun','nasr','masad','fil','quraysh','maun','sharh','tin','bayyina','zilzal','kawthar','asr'];
  const AYAH_COUNTS={fatiha:7,ikhlas:4,falaq:5,nas:6,kafirun:6,nasr:3,masad:5,fil:5,quraysh:4,maun:7,sharh:8,tin:8,bayyina:8,zilzal:8,kawthar:3,asr:3};
  const AYAH_STATES=['new','learning','strengthening','solid'];
  const SURAH_STATES=['not_started','learning','memorized','review_due'];

  const nowIso=()=>new Date().toISOString();
  const todayKey=()=>{
    const d=new Date();
    const y=d.getFullYear();
    const m=String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0');
    const day=String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0');
    return y+'-'+m+'-'+day;
  };
  const isObj=v=>!!v&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v);
  const cleanId=id=>SURAH_IDS.includes(id)?id:'fatiha';
  const num=(v,fallback=0)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):fallback;
  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.min(max,Math.max(min,num(v,min)));
  const safeIso=v=>{
    if(typeof v!=='string'||!v)return null;
    const t=Date.parse(v);
    return Number.isFinite(t)?new Date(t).toISOString():null;
  };

  function blankAyah(){
    return {
      state:'new',
      memorizationLevel:0,
      repetitions:0,
      correctRecalls:0,
      wrongRecalls:0,
      lastStudiedAt:null,
      nextReviewAt:null,
      completedAt:null
    };
  }

  function normalizeAyah(raw){
    const a=isObj(raw)?raw:{};
    const out={...blankAyah(),...a};
    if(!AYAH_STATES.includes(out.state))out.state='new';
    out.memorizationLevel=clamp(out.memorizationLevel,0,5);
    out.repetitions=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(out.repetitions,0)));
    out.correctRecalls=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(out.correctRecalls,0)));
    out.wrongRecalls=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(out.wrongRecalls,0)));
    out.lastStudiedAt=safeIso(out.lastStudiedAt);
    out.nextReviewAt=safeIso(out.nextReviewAt);
    out.completedAt=safeIso(out.completedAt);
    return out;
  }

  function blankSurah(id){
    const ayahs={};
    for(let i=1;i<=AYAH_COUNTS[id];i++)ayahs[i]=blankAyah();
    return {
      status:'not_started',
      startedAt:null,
      lastStudiedAt:null,
      memorizedAt:null,
      nextReviewAt:null,
      currentAyah:1,
      completedAyahs:0,
      ayahs
    };
  }

  function normalizeSurah(id,raw){
    const base=blankSurah(id);
    const s=isObj(raw)?raw:{};
    const out={...base,...s};
    if(!SURAH_STATES.includes(out.status))out.status='not_started';
    out.startedAt=safeIso(out.startedAt);
    out.lastStudiedAt=safeIso(out.lastStudiedAt);
    out.memorizedAt=safeIso(out.memorizedAt);
    out.nextReviewAt=safeIso(out.nextReviewAt);
    out.currentAyah=Math.round(clamp(out.currentAyah,1,AYAH_COUNTS[id]));
    out.ayahs={};
    let completed=0;
    for(let i=1;i<=AYAH_COUNTS[id];i++){
      out.ayahs[i]=normalizeAyah(s.ayahs?.[i]);
      if(out.ayahs[i].state==='solid'||out.ayahs[i].completedAt)completed++;
    }
    out.completedAyahs=completed;
    return out;
  }

  function normalizeReviewItem(item){
    if(!isObj(item))return null;
    const surahId=cleanId(item.surahId||item.surah||item.surah_id);
    const ayah=Math.round(clamp(item.ayah||item.ayahNumber||1,1,AYAH_COUNTS[surahId]));
    return {
      id:String(item.id||surahId+':'+ayah),
      surahId,
      ayah,
      dueAt:safeIso(item.dueAt||item.nextReviewAt)||nowIso(),
      reason:String(item.reason||'scheduled'),
      createdAt:safeIso(item.createdAt)||nowIso()
    };
  }

  function ensure(){
    state.quranJourney=isObj(state.quranJourney)?state.quranJourney:{};
    const q=state.quranJourney;
    const oldVersion=Math.max(1,Math.floor(num(q.schemaVersion||q.version,1)));
    const migratedAt=safeIso(q.meta?.migratedAt)||nowIso();

    q.progress=isObj(q.progress)?q.progress:{};
    q.progress.surahs=isObj(q.progress.surahs)?q.progress.surahs:{};
    q.startedSurahs=Array.isArray(q.startedSurahs)?q.startedSurahs.filter(x=>SURAH_IDS.includes(x)):[];
    q.memorizedSurahs=Array.isArray(q.memorizedSurahs)?q.memorizedSurahs.filter(x=>SURAH_IDS.includes(x)):[];
    q.currentSurah=cleanId(q.currentSurah);
    const legacyVerseLearned=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(q.verseLearned,0)));

    for(const id of SURAH_IDS){
      const s=normalizeSurah(id,q.progress.surahs[id]);
      if(q.startedSurahs.includes(id)&&s.status==='not_started'){
        s.status='learning';
        s.startedAt=s.startedAt||migratedAt;
      }
      if(q.memorizedSurahs.includes(id)){
        s.status='memorized';
        s.startedAt=s.startedAt||migratedAt;
        s.memorizedAt=s.memorizedAt||migratedAt;
      }
      q.progress.surahs[id]=s;
    }

    q.profile=isObj(q.profile)?q.profile:{};
    q.profile.currentSurah=cleanId(q.profile.currentSurah||q.currentSurah);
    q.profile.currentAyah=Math.round(clamp(q.profile.currentAyah||q.progress.surahs[q.profile.currentSurah].currentAyah,1,AYAH_COUNTS[q.profile.currentSurah]));
    q.profile.lastMode=typeof q.profile.lastMode==='string'?q.profile.lastMode:'learn';
    q.profile.lastSessionAt=safeIso(q.profile.lastSessionAt);
    q.profile.lastSession=isObj(q.profile.lastSession)?q.profile.lastSession:null;

    q.goals=isObj(q.goals)?q.goals:{};
    q.goals.dailyAyahs=Math.round(clamp(q.goals.dailyAyahs??1,1,20));
    q.goals.dailyMinutes=Math.round(clamp(q.goals.dailyMinutes??5,1,120));
    q.goals.weeklyDays=Math.round(clamp(q.goals.weeklyDays??3,1,7));
    q.goals.primary=['ayahs','minutes','days'].includes(q.goals.primary)?q.goals.primary:'ayahs';

    q.daily=isObj(q.daily)?q.daily:{};
    if(q.daily.date!==todayKey()){
      q.daily={date:todayKey(),minutes:0,ayahs:0,sessions:0,lastSessionAt:null};
    }else{
      q.daily.minutes=Math.max(0,num(q.daily.minutes,0));
      q.daily.ayahs=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(q.daily.ayahs,0)));
      q.daily.sessions=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(q.daily.sessions,0)));
      q.daily.lastSessionAt=safeIso(q.daily.lastSessionAt);
    }

    q.favorites=isObj(q.favorites)?q.favorites:{};
    q.favorites.ayahs=Array.isArray(q.favorites.ayahs)?q.favorites.ayahs.filter(x=>typeof x==='string'):[];
    q.favorites.surahs=Array.isArray(q.favorites.surahs)?q.favorites.surahs.filter(x=>SURAH_IDS.includes(x)):[];

    q.review=isObj(q.review)?q.review:{};
    const legacyQueue=Array.isArray(q.reviewQueue)?q.reviewQueue:[];
    const sourceQueue=Array.isArray(q.review.queue)&&q.review.queue.length?q.review.queue:legacyQueue;
    const normalizedQueue=[];
    const unmappedLegacy=[];
    for(const item of sourceQueue){
      const normalized=normalizeReviewItem(item);
      if(normalized)normalizedQueue.push(normalized); else unmappedLegacy.push(item);
    }
    q.review.queue=normalizedQueue;
    q.review.legacyQueue=Array.isArray(q.review.legacyQueue)?q.review.legacyQueue:[];
    if(unmappedLegacy.length)q.review.legacyQueue.push(...unmappedLegacy);
    q.review.history=Array.isArray(q.review.history)?q.review.history.filter(isObj):[];
    q.review.lastRunAt=safeIso(q.review.lastRunAt);

    q.meta=isObj(q.meta)?q.meta:{};
    q.meta.schemaVersion=SCHEMA_VERSION;
    const previousMigratedFrom=Math.max(1,Math.floor(num(q.meta.migratedFrom,oldVersion)));
    q.meta.migratedFrom=Math.min(previousMigratedFrom,oldVersion,SCHEMA_VERSION);
    q.meta.migratedAt=q.meta.migratedAt||migratedAt;
    q.meta.updatedAt=nowIso();

    q.schemaVersion=SCHEMA_VERSION;
    q.version=SCHEMA_VERSION;
    q.currentSurah=q.profile.currentSurah;
    q.reviewQueue=q.review.queue;
    const structuredVerseLearned=SURAH_IDS.reduce((sum,id)=>sum+q.progress.surahs[id].completedAyahs,0);
    q.progress.legacyVerseLearned=Math.max(legacyVerseLearned,Math.floor(num(q.progress.legacyVerseLearned,0)));
    q.verseLearned=Math.max(q.progress.legacyVerseLearned,structuredVerseLearned);
    q.startedSurahs=SURAH_IDS.filter(id=>q.progress.surahs[id].status!=='not_started');
    q.memorizedSurahs=SURAH_IDS.filter(id=>q.progress.surahs[id].status==='memorized');
    return q;
  }

  function persist(){
    const q=ensure();
    q.meta.updatedAt=nowIso();
    try{saveState?.()}catch(_){}
    return q;
  }

  function startSurah(id,ayah=1,mode='learn'){
    const q=ensure();
    id=cleanId(id);
    const s=q.progress.surahs[id];
    const at=nowIso();
    if(s.status==='not_started')s.status='learning';
    s.startedAt=s.startedAt||at;
    s.lastStudiedAt=at;
    s.currentAyah=Math.round(clamp(ayah,1,AYAH_COUNTS[id]));
    q.profile.currentSurah=id;
    q.profile.currentAyah=s.currentAyah;
    q.profile.lastMode=String(mode||'learn');
    q.profile.lastSessionAt=at;
    q.profile.lastSession={surahId:id,ayah:s.currentAyah,mode:q.profile.lastMode,at};
    q.daily.sessions+=1;
    q.daily.lastSessionAt=at;
    return persist();
  }

  function updateAyah(id,ayah,changes={}){
    const q=ensure();
    id=cleanId(id);
    ayah=Math.round(clamp(ayah,1,AYAH_COUNTS[id]));
    const s=q.progress.surahs[id];
    const current=normalizeAyah(s.ayahs[ayah]);
    const next=normalizeAyah({...current,...(isObj(changes)?changes:{})});
    next.lastStudiedAt=safeIso(changes.lastStudiedAt)||next.lastStudiedAt||nowIso();
    s.ayahs[ayah]=next;
    s.lastStudiedAt=next.lastStudiedAt;
    if(s.status==='not_started')s.status='learning';
    if(!s.startedAt)s.startedAt=next.lastStudiedAt;
    q.profile.currentSurah=id;
    q.profile.currentAyah=ayah;
    return persist();
  }

  function scheduleReview(id,ayah,dueAt,reason='scheduled'){
    const q=ensure();
    id=cleanId(id);
    ayah=Math.round(clamp(ayah,1,AYAH_COUNTS[id]));
    const due=safeIso(dueAt)||nowIso();
    const key=id+':'+ayah;
    q.review.queue=q.review.queue.filter(x=>x.id!==key);
    q.review.queue.push({id:key,surahId:id,ayah,dueAt:due,reason:String(reason),createdAt:nowIso()});
    q.review.queue.sort((a,b)=>Date.parse(a.dueAt)-Date.parse(b.dueAt));
    q.progress.surahs[id].ayahs[ayah].nextReviewAt=due;
    q.progress.surahs[id].nextReviewAt=q.review.queue.find(x=>x.surahId===id)?.dueAt||due;
    q.reviewQueue=q.review.queue;
    return persist();
  }

  function toggleFavorite(id,ayah){
    const q=ensure();
    id=cleanId(id);
    const key=ayah==null?id:id+':'+Math.round(clamp(ayah,1,AYAH_COUNTS[id]));
    const bucket=ayah==null?q.favorites.surahs:q.favorites.ayahs;
    const pos=bucket.indexOf(key);
    if(pos>=0)bucket.splice(pos,1); else bucket.push(key);
    persist();
    return pos<0;
  }

  function setGoal(goal,value){
    const q=ensure();
    if(goal==='dailyAyahs')q.goals.dailyAyahs=Math.round(clamp(value,1,20));
    else if(goal==='dailyMinutes')q.goals.dailyMinutes=Math.round(clamp(value,1,120));
    else if(goal==='weeklyDays')q.goals.weeklyDays=Math.round(clamp(value,1,7));
    else if(goal==='primary'&&['ayahs','minutes','days'].includes(value))q.goals.primary=value;
    return persist();
  }

  function snapshot(){
    return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ensure()));
  }

  const base=window.DEEN_QURAN||{};
  const baseOpen=typeof base.openSurah==='function'?base.openSurah.bind(base):null;
  const baseRender=typeof base.render==='function'?base.render.bind(base):null;

  if(baseOpen){
    base.openSurah=function(id){
      const q=ensure();
      id=cleanId(id);
      const ayah=q.progress.surahs[id]?.currentAyah||q.profile.currentAyah||1;
      startSurah(id,ayah,'learn');
      const out=baseOpen(id);
      const restored=ensure();
      restored.profile.currentSurah=id;
      restored.profile.currentAyah=ayah;
      restored.progress.surahs[id].currentAyah=ayah;
      persist();
      return out;
    };
  }
  if(baseRender){
    base.render=function(){
      ensure();
      return baseRender();
    };
  }

  base.schemaVersion=SCHEMA_VERSION;
  base.ensureState=ensure;
  base.persistState=persist;
  base.startSurah=startSurah;
  base.updateAyah=updateAyah;
  base.scheduleReview=scheduleReview;
  base.toggleFavorite=toggleFavorite;
  base.setGoal=setGoal;
  base.state=snapshot;
  base.schema=()=>({
    version:SCHEMA_VERSION,
    surahStates:[...SURAH_STATES],
    ayahStates:[...AYAH_STATES],
    surahs:SURAH_IDS.map(id=>({id,ayahs:AYAH_COUNTS[id]}))
  });
  base.checkData=()=>{
    const q=ensure();
    return {
      schemaVersion:q.schemaVersion,
      currentSurah:q.profile.currentSurah,
      currentAyah:q.profile.currentAyah,
      startedSurahs:q.startedSurahs.length,
      memorizedSurahs:q.memorizedSurahs.length,
      reviewItems:q.review.queue.length,
      favorites:q.favorites.ayahs.length+q.favorites.surahs.length,
      dailyGoal:{ayahs:q.goals.dailyAyahs,minutes:q.goals.dailyMinutes,weeklyDays:q.goals.weeklyDays},
      legacyCompatible:Array.isArray(q.startedSurahs)&&Array.isArray(q.memorizedSurahs)&&Array.isArray(q.reviewQueue)
    };
  };
  window.DEEN_QURAN=base;

  const migrated=ensure();
  if(migrated.meta.migratedFrom<SCHEMA_VERSION||!migrated.meta.persistedSchemaV2){
    migrated.meta.persistedSchemaV2=true;
    persist();
  }
  document.documentElement.dataset.deenQuranData='v10917';
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v10918-quran-dashboard-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10918QuranDashboard)return;
  window.__deenV10918QuranDashboard=true;

  const META={
    fatiha:{name:'Fâtiha',ayahs:7},
    ikhlas:{name:'İhlâs',ayahs:4},
    falaq:{name:'Felak',ayahs:5},
    nas:{name:'Nâs',ayahs:6},
    kafirun:{name:'Kâfirûn',ayahs:6},
    nasr:{name:'Nasr',ayahs:3},
    masad:{name:'Tebbet',ayahs:5},
    fil:{name:'Fîl',ayahs:5},
    quraysh:{name:'Kureyş',ayahs:4},
    maun:{name:'Mâûn',ayahs:7},
    sharh:{name:'İnşirah',ayahs:8},
    tin:{name:'Tîn',ayahs:8},
    bayyina:{name:'Beyyine',ayahs:8},
    zilzal:{name:'Zilzâl',ayahs:8},
    kawthar:{name:'Kevser',ayahs:3},
    asr:{name:'Asr',ayahs:3}
  };
  const IDS=Object.keys(META);
  const TOTAL_AYAHS=IDS.reduce((n,id)=>n+META[id].ayahs,0);

  const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));
  const clampPct=n=>Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(Number(n)||0)));
  const dateKey=d=>d.getFullYear()+'-'+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0');

  function qstate(){
    try{
      if(typeof DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState==='function')return DEEN_QURAN.ensureState();
      if(typeof DEEN_QURAN?.state==='function')return DEEN_QURAN.state();
    }catch(_){}
    return state?.quranJourney||{};
  }
  function surahName(id){return META[id]?.name||'Fâtiha'}
  function humanDate(iso){
    if(!iso)return 'Henüz çalışma yok';
    const d=new Date(iso);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d.getTime()))return 'Henüz çalışma yok';
    const now=new Date();
    const today=dateKey(now);
    const yesterday=new Date(now); yesterday.setDate(now.getDate()-1);
    if(dateKey(d)===today)return 'Bugün';
    if(dateKey(d)===dateKey(yesterday))return 'Dün';
    return d.toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'numeric',month:'short'});
  }
  function statusLabel(status){
    return ({
      not_started:'Başlanmadı',
      learning:'Öğreniliyor',
      memorized:'Ezberlendi',
      review_due:'Tekrar zamanı'
    })[status]||'Öğreniliyor';
  }
  function dueToday(queue){
    const now=Date.now();
    const end=new Date(); end.setHours(23,59,59,999);
    const todayEnd=end.getTime();
    return (Array.isArray(queue)?queue:[]).filter(item=>{
      const t=Date.parse(item?.dueAt);
      return Number.isFinite(t)&&t<=todayEnd;
    }).sort((a,b)=>Date.parse(a.dueAt)-Date.parse(b.dueAt));
  }
  function model(){
    const q=qstate();
    const fallbackId=IDS.includes(q?.profile?.currentSurah)?q.profile.currentSurah:'fatiha';
    const lastId=IDS.includes(q?.profile?.lastSession?.surahId)?q.profile.lastSession.surahId:fallbackId;
    const meta=META[lastId]||META.fatiha;
    const progress=q?.progress?.surahs?.[lastId]||{};
    const currentAyah=Math.max(1,Math.min(meta.ayahs,Number(q?.profile?.currentAyah||progress.currentAyah||1)));
    const completed=Math.max(0,Number(progress.completedAyahs)||0);
    const surahPct=clampPct((completed/meta.ayahs)*100);
    const structured=IDS.reduce((sum,id)=>sum+Math.max(0,Number(q?.progress?.surahs?.[id]?.completedAyahs)||0),0);
    const learned=Math.min(TOTAL_AYAHS,Math.max(structured,Number(q?.verseLearned)||0));
    const overallPct=clampPct((learned/TOTAL_AYAHS)*100);
    const due=dueToday(q?.review?.queue||q?.reviewQueue);
    const overdue=due.filter(x=>Date.parse(x.dueAt)<Date.now()).length;
    const primary=q?.goals?.primary||'ayahs';
    let goalValue=Number(q?.daily?.ayahs)||0;
    let goalTarget=Math.max(1,Number(q?.goals?.dailyAyahs)||1);
    let goalUnit='ayet';
    let goalLabel='Günlük ayet hedefi';
    if(primary==='minutes'){
      goalValue=Number(q?.daily?.minutes)||0;
      goalTarget=Math.max(1,Number(q?.goals?.dailyMinutes)||5);
      goalUnit='dk';
      goalLabel='Günlük çalışma hedefi';
    }else if(primary==='days'){
      goalValue=(Number(q?.daily?.sessions)||0)>0?1:0;
      goalTarget=1;
      goalUnit='gün';
      goalLabel='Bugünkü çalışma';
    }
    const goalPct=clampPct((goalValue/goalTarget)*100);
    const started=Array.isArray(q?.startedSurahs)?q.startedSurahs.length:0;
    const memorized=Array.isArray(q?.memorizedSurahs)?q.memorizedSurahs.length:0;
    const lastAt=q?.profile?.lastSessionAt||progress.lastStudiedAt||null;
    return {
      currentId:lastId,
      currentName:surahName(lastId),
      currentAyah,
      ayahs:meta.ayahs,
      completed,
      surahPct,
      status:progress.status||'not_started',
      started,
      memorized,
      learned,
      totalAyahs:TOTAL_AYAHS,
      overallPct,
      dueCount:due.length,
      overdue,
      goalValue,
      goalTarget,
      goalUnit,
      goalLabel,
      goalPct,
      lastAt,
      hasStarted:started>0
    };
  }
  function render(){
    try{DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState?.()}catch(_){}
    const root=document.getElementById('leagueScreenBody');
    if(!root)return false;
    const m=model();
    const today=new Date().toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'numeric',month:'short'});
    const cta=m.hasStarted?'ÇALIŞMAYA DEVAM ET':m.currentName.toUpperCase()+' İLE BAŞLA';
    const reviewCopy=m.dueCount
      ? (m.overdue?m.overdue+' gecikmiş tekrar dahil':'Bugün tamamlanması gerekenler')
      : 'Bugün için planlanmış tekrar yok';
    root.innerHTML=
      '<div class="deen-quran-shell deen-quran-dashboard">'+
        '<div class="deen-quran-head">'+
          '<div><div class="deen-quran-kicker">KUR’AN ÖĞRENME MERKEZİ</div>'+
          '<h1 class="deen-quran-title">Kur’an Yolculuğun</h1>'+
          '<p class="deen-quran-sub">Kaldığın yerden devam et, bugünkü tekrarlarını tamamla ve ilerlemeni sakin bir ritimde güçlendir.</p></div>'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-date">'+esc(today)+'</div>'+
        '</div>'+

        '<section class="deen-quran-hero">'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-hero-top">'+
            '<span class="deen-quran-live-badge">'+(m.hasStarted?'DEVAM ET':'İLK ADIM')+'</span>'+
            '<span class="deen-quran-status-pill">'+esc(statusLabel(m.status))+'</span>'+
          '</div>'+
          '<h2>'+esc(m.currentName)+' Suresi</h2>'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-hero-meta">Ayet '+m.currentAyah+' / '+m.ayahs+' · Son çalışma: '+esc(humanDate(m.lastAt))+'</div>'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-progress-row"><span>Sure ilerlemesi</span><strong>%'+m.surahPct+'</strong></div>'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-progress"><i style="width:'+m.surahPct+'%"></i></div>'+
          '<button class="deen-quran-primary" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.openSurah(\''+esc(m.currentId)+'\')">'+esc(cta)+'</button>'+
        '</section>'+

        '<div class="deen-quran-today-grid">'+
          '<section class="deen-quran-today-card">'+
            '<div class="top"><span class="label">BUGÜNKÜ HEDEF</span><span class="icon">✓</span></div>'+
            '<strong>'+m.goalValue+' / '+m.goalTarget+' '+esc(m.goalUnit)+'</strong>'+
            '<small>'+esc(m.goalLabel)+(m.goalPct>=100?' tamamlandı.':' · düzenli küçük adımlar yeterli.')+'</small>'+
            '<div class="deen-quran-mini-progress"><i style="width:'+m.goalPct+'%"></i></div>'+
          '</section>'+
          '<section class="deen-quran-today-card">'+
            '<div class="top"><span class="label">BUGÜNKÜ TEKRARLAR</span><span class="icon">↻</span></div>'+
            '<strong>'+m.dueCount+'</strong>'+
            '<small>'+esc(reviewCopy)+'</small>'+
            '<button class="deen-quran-review-link" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.review()">'+(m.dueCount?'TEKRARLARI AÇ':'TEKRAR PLANINI GÖR')+'</button>'+
          '</section>'+
        '</div>'+

        '<section class="deen-quran-overview">'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-overview-head"><b>Genel ilerleme</b><span>%'+m.overallPct+'</span></div>'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-progress"><i style="width:'+m.overallPct+'%"></i></div>'+
          '<div class="deen-quran-overview-metrics">'+
            '<div><strong>'+m.learned+'</strong><small>ÖĞRENİLEN AYET</small></div>'+
            '<div><strong>'+m.started+'</strong><small>BAŞLANAN SURE</small></div>'+
            '<div><strong>'+m.memorized+'</strong><small>EZBERLENEN SURE</small></div>'+
          '</div>'+
        '</section>'+

        '<h2 class="deen-quran-section-title">Çalışma alanları</h2>'+
        '<div class="deen-quran-quick">'+
          '<button type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.surahList()"><i>☰</i><b>Sureler</b><small>Öğrenme kütüphanesi</small></button>'+
          '<button type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.memorize()"><i>◌</i><b>Ezber</b><small>Hatırlama çalışması</small></button>'+
          '<button type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.dailyVerse()"><i>✦</i><b>Bugünün Ayeti</b><small>Günlük anlam çalışması</small></button>'+
        '</div>'+
        '<p class="deen-quran-note">Kur’an metni ve meal kaynakları kayıt altında tutulur; uzman onayı production release gate üzerinden ayrıca izlenir.</p>'+
      '</div>';
    document.documentElement.dataset.deenQuranDashboard='v10918';
    return true;
  }

  window.DEEN_QURAN=window.DEEN_QURAN||{};
  window.DEEN_QURAN.dashboardModel=model;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.dashboardRender=render;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.render=render;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.checkDashboard=()=>{
    const m=model();
    return {
      body:!!document.querySelector('#leagueScreenBody .deen-quran-dashboard'),
      current:m.currentId,
      currentAyah:m.currentAyah,
      dueToday:m.dueCount,
      goalPct:m.goalPct,
      overallPct:m.overallPct
    };
  };
  window.leagueView=render;
  try{leagueView=render}catch(_){}

  document.addEventListener('deen:navigated',()=>{
    if(document.querySelector('.screen.active')?.id==='leagueScreen')render();
  });
  if(document.querySelector('.screen.active')?.id==='leagueScreen')render();
})();
</script>
