<script id="deen-v10919-quran-library-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10919QuranLibrary)return;
  window.__deenV10919QuranLibrary=true;

  const LIBRARY=[
    {id:'fatiha',name:'Fâtiha',order:1,ayahs:7},
    {id:'ikhlas',name:'İhlâs',order:112,ayahs:4},
    {id:'falaq',name:'Felak',order:113,ayahs:5},
    {id:'nas',name:'Nâs',order:114,ayahs:6},
    {id:'kafirun',name:'Kâfirûn',order:109,ayahs:6},
    {id:'nasr',name:'Nasr',order:110,ayahs:3},
    {id:'masad',name:'Tebbet',order:111,ayahs:5},
    {id:'fil',name:'Fîl',order:105,ayahs:5},
    {id:'quraysh',name:'Kureyş',order:106,ayahs:4},
    {id:'maun',name:'Mâûn',order:107,ayahs:7},
    {id:'sharh',name:'İnşirah',order:94,ayahs:8},
    {id:'tin',name:'Tîn',order:95,ayahs:8},
    {id:'bayyina',name:'Beyyine',order:98,ayahs:8},
    {id:'kawthar',name:'Kevser',order:108,ayahs:3},
    {id:'asr',name:'Asr',order:103,ayahs:3}
  ];

  const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));
  const pct=n=>Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(Number(n)||0)));

  function stateNow(){
    try{
      if(typeof DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState==='function')return DEEN_QURAN.ensureState();
      if(typeof DEEN_QURAN?.state==='function')return DEEN_QURAN.state();
    }catch(_){}
    return state?.quranJourney||{};
  }
  function dueForSurah(q,id){
    const end=new Date();
    end.setHours(23,59,59,999);
    const max=end.getTime();
    return (q?.review?.queue||q?.reviewQueue||[]).some(item=>{
      if(item?.surahId!==id)return false;
      const t=Date.parse(item?.dueAt);
      return Number.isFinite(t)&&t<=max;
    });
  }
  function status(q,id,s){
    if(dueForSurah(q,id))return 'review_due';
    if(s?.status==='memorized')return 'memorized';
    if(s?.status==='learning'||s?.startedAt)return 'learning';
    return 'not_started';
  }
  function label(st){
    return ({
      not_started:'BAŞLANMADI',
      learning:'ÖĞRENİLİYOR',
      memorized:'EZBERLENDİ',
      review_due:'TEKRAR ZAMANI'
    })[st]||'BAŞLANMADI';
  }
  function when(iso){
    if(!iso)return 'Henüz çalışılmadı';
    const d=new Date(iso);
    if(!Number.isFinite(d.getTime()))return 'Henüz çalışılmadı';
    const now=new Date();
    const key=x=>x.getFullYear()+'-'+String(x.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(x.getDate()).padStart(2,'0');
    if(key(d)===key(now))return 'Bugün çalışıldı';
    const y=new Date(now); y.setDate(now.getDate()-1);
    if(key(d)===key(y))return 'Dün çalışıldı';
    return d.toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'numeric',month:'short'})+' çalışıldı';
  }
  function rows(){
    const q=stateNow();
    const current=q?.profile?.currentSurah||q?.currentSurah||'fatiha';
    return LIBRARY.map(meta=>{
      const s=q?.progress?.surahs?.[meta.id]||{};
      const completed=Math.max(0,Math.min(meta.ayahs,Number(s.completedAyahs)||0));
      const currentAyah=Math.max(1,Math.min(meta.ayahs,Number(s.currentAyah)||1));
      const st=status(q,meta.id,s);
      return {
        ...meta,
        completed,
        currentAyah,
        progress:pct((completed/meta.ayahs)*100),
        status:st,
        current:current===meta.id,
        lastStudiedAt:s.lastStudiedAt||null
      };
    });
  }
  function render(){
    const items=rows();
    const started=items.filter(x=>x.status!=='not_started').length;
    const memorized=items.filter(x=>x.status==='memorized').length;
    const due=items.filter(x=>x.status==='review_due').length;

    sheet('Sure Kütüphanesi',
      '<div class="v5-kicker">16 SURE · ÖĞRENME & EZBER</div>'+
      '<p class="v5-muted">Her surenin ilerlemesi ve tekrar durumu ayrı tutulur. Bir sureyi seçtiğinde kaldığın ayetten devam edersin.</p>'+
      '<div class="deen-quran-library-summary">'+
        '<div><strong>'+started+'</strong><small>BAŞLANAN</small></div>'+
        '<div><strong>'+memorized+'</strong><small>EZBERLENEN</small></div>'+
        '<div><strong>'+due+'</strong><small>TEKRAR ZAMANI</small></div>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-quran-library-wrap">'+
        items.map(item=>
          '<button type="button" class="deen-quran-surah-card'+(item.current?' is-current':'')+'" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.openSurah(\''+esc(item.id)+'\')">'+
            '<div class="deen-quran-surah-top">'+
              '<span class="deen-quran-surah-name">'+
                '<b>'+esc(item.name)+' Suresi</b>'+
                '<small>'+item.order+'. sure · '+item.ayahs+' ayet'+(item.current?' · Şu an buradasın':'')+'</small>'+
              '</span>'+
              '<span class="deen-quran-surah-status '+item.status+'">'+esc(label(item.status))+'</span>'+
            '</div>'+
            '<div class="deen-quran-surah-progress-row">'+
              '<span>'+item.completed+' / '+item.ayahs+' ayet tamamlandı</span>'+
              '<strong>%'+item.progress+'</strong>'+
            '</div>'+
            '<div class="deen-quran-surah-bar"><i style="width:'+item.progress+'%"></i></div>'+
            '<div class="deen-quran-surah-foot">'+
              '<span>'+esc(when(item.lastStudiedAt))+'</span>'+
              '<em>'+(item.status==='not_started'?'Başla':'Ayet '+item.currentAyah+' →')+'</em>'+
            '</div>'+
          '</button>'
        ).join('')+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-quran-library-note">Arapça metin, meal ve ses kaynakları ayrı izlenir; uzman release onayı admin doğrulama hattında yönetilir.</div>'
    );
    document.documentElement.dataset.deenQuranLibrary='v10919';
    return true;
  }

  window.DEEN_QURAN=window.DEEN_QURAN||{};
  window.DEEN_QURAN.surahList=render;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.libraryRows=rows;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.checkLibrary=()=>{
    const r=rows();
    return {
      count:r.length,
      current:r.find(x=>x.current)?.id||null,
      statuses:r.reduce((acc,x)=>{acc[x.id]=x.status;return acc;},{}),
      progress:r.reduce((acc,x)=>{acc[x.id]=x.progress;return acc;},{})
    };
  };
})();
</script>


<!-- DEEN v10.9.20 — verified Quran content registry and production gate -->
<script id="deen-v10920-quran-content-registry-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10920QuranContentRegistry)return;
  window.__deenV10920QuranContentRegistry=true;

  const SCHEMA_VERSION=1;
  const STATUS=['draft','needs_review','verified','production_ready','rejected'];
  const SURAH_META={
    fatiha:{number:1,name:'Fâtiha',ayahs:7},
    asr:{number:103,name:'Asr',ayahs:3},
    kawthar:{number:108,name:'Kevser',ayahs:3},
    kafirun:{number:109,name:'Kâfirûn',ayahs:6},
    nasr:{number:110,name:'Nasr',ayahs:3},
    masad:{number:111,name:'Tebbet',ayahs:5},
    fil:{number:105,name:'Fîl',ayahs:5},
    quraysh:{number:106,name:'Kureyş',ayahs:4},
    maun:{number:107,name:'Mâûn',ayahs:7},
    sharh:{number:94,name:'İnşirah',ayahs:8},
    tin:{number:95,name:'Tîn',ayahs:8},
    bayyina:{number:98,name:'Beyyine',ayahs:8},
    zilzal:{number:99,name:'Zilzâl',ayahs:8},
    ikhlas:{number:112,name:'İhlâs',ayahs:4},
    falaq:{number:113,name:'Felak',ayahs:5},
    nas:{number:114,name:'Nâs',ayahs:6}
  };

  const store={};
  const isObj=v=>!!v&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v);
  const clone=v=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));
  const trim=v=>typeof v==='string'?v.trim():'';
  const iso=v=>{
    if(typeof v!=='string'||!v)return null;
    const t=Date.parse(v);
    return Number.isFinite(t)?new Date(t).toISOString():null;
  };
  const cleanStatus=v=>STATUS.includes(v)?v:'needs_review';
  const validSurah=id=>Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(SURAH_META,id);
  const ayahCount=id=>SURAH_META[id]?.ayahs||0;
  const validAyah=(id,n)=>validSurah(id)&&Number.isInteger(Number(n))&&Number(n)>=1&&Number(n)<=ayahCount(id);
  const key=(id,n)=>id+':'+Number(n);

  function blankSource(){
    return {
      provider:null,
      edition:null,
      reference:null,
      url:null,
      license:null,
      rights_reference:null
    };
  }
  function normalizeSource(raw){
    const s=isObj(raw)?raw:{};
    return {
      provider:trim(s.provider)||null,
      edition:trim(s.edition)||null,
      reference:trim(s.reference)||null,
      url:trim(s.url)||null,
      license:trim(s.license)||null,
      rights_reference:trim(s.rights_reference)||null
    };
  }
  function blankVerification(){
    return {
      verification_status:'needs_review',
      religious_release_ready:false,
      verified_by:null,
      verified_at:null,
      review_notes:[]
    };
  }
  function normalizeVerification(raw){
    const v=isObj(raw)?raw:{};
    return {
      verification_status:cleanStatus(v.verification_status),
      religious_release_ready:v.religious_release_ready===true,
      verified_by:trim(v.verified_by)||null,
      verified_at:iso(v.verified_at),
      review_notes:Array.isArray(v.review_notes)?v.review_notes.map(x=>trim(x)).filter(Boolean):[]
    };
  }
  function blankAyah(id,n){
    const meta=SURAH_META[id];
    return {
      schema_version:SCHEMA_VERSION,
      surah_id:id,
      surah_number:meta.number,
      surah_name:meta.name,
      ayah_number:Number(n),
      arabic:{
        text:null,
        source:blankSource(),
        verification_status:'needs_review'
      },
      translation:{
        language:'tr',
        text:null,
        source:blankSource(),
        verification_status:'needs_review'
      },
      recitation:{
        audio_url:null,
        qari:null,
        riwayah:null,
        source:blankSource(),
        verification_status:'needs_review',
        rights_status:'unknown'
      },
      learning_note:{
        text:null,
        source:blankSource(),
        verification_status:'needs_review'
      },
      verification:blankVerification(),
      updated_at:new Date().toISOString()
    };
  }
  function normalizeAyah(raw){
    if(!isObj(raw))throw new Error('Kur’an içerik kaydı nesne olmalı');
    const id=trim(raw.surah_id);
    const n=Number(raw.ayah_number);
    if(!validAyah(id,n))throw new Error('Geçersiz sure/ayet anahtarı');
    const base=blankAyah(id,n);
    const arabic=isObj(raw.arabic)?raw.arabic:{};
    const translation=isObj(raw.translation)?raw.translation:{};
    const recitation=isObj(raw.recitation)?raw.recitation:{};
    const learning=isObj(raw.learning_note)?raw.learning_note:{};
    return {
      ...base,
      arabic:{
        text:trim(arabic.text)||null,
        source:normalizeSource(arabic.source),
        verification_status:cleanStatus(arabic.verification_status)
      },
      translation:{
        language:trim(translation.language)||'tr',
        text:trim(translation.text)||null,
        source:normalizeSource(translation.source),
        verification_status:cleanStatus(translation.verification_status)
      },
      recitation:{
        audio_url:trim(recitation.audio_url)||null,
        qari:trim(recitation.qari)||null,
        riwayah:trim(recitation.riwayah)||null,
        source:normalizeSource(recitation.source),
        verification_status:cleanStatus(recitation.verification_status),
        rights_status:['unknown','cleared','restricted'].includes(recitation.rights_status)?recitation.rights_status:'unknown'
      },
      learning_note:{
        text:trim(learning.text)||null,
        source:normalizeSource(learning.source),
        verification_status:cleanStatus(learning.verification_status)
      },
      verification:normalizeVerification(raw.verification),
      updated_at:iso(raw.updated_at)||new Date().toISOString()
    };
  }

  function sourcePresent(src){
    return !!(src&&(src.provider||src.reference||src.url||src.edition));
  }
  function sourceGate(record){
    const reasons=[];
    if(!record)reasons.push('missing_record');
    if(!record)return {ready:false,reasons};

    if(!record.arabic?.text)reasons.push('arabic_text_missing');
    if(!sourcePresent(record.arabic?.source))reasons.push('arabic_source_missing');
    if(record.arabic?.verification_status!=='production_ready')reasons.push('arabic_not_source_ready');

    if(!record.translation?.text)reasons.push('translation_text_missing');
    if(!sourcePresent(record.translation?.source))reasons.push('translation_source_missing');
    if(record.translation?.verification_status!=='production_ready')reasons.push('translation_not_source_ready');

    return {ready:reasons.length===0,reasons};
  }
  function expertReleaseGate(record){
    const source=sourceGate(record);
    const reasons=[...source.reasons];
    if(!record)return {ready:false,reasons};
    if(record.verification?.religious_release_ready!==true)reasons.push('religious_release_ready_false');
    if(record.verification?.verification_status!=='production_ready')reasons.push('record_not_production_ready');
    if(!record.verification?.verified_by)reasons.push('verified_by_missing');
    if(!record.verification?.verified_at)reasons.push('verified_at_missing');
    return {ready:reasons.length===0,reasons};
  }
  function recitationGate(record){
    const reasons=[];
    if(!record?.recitation?.audio_url)reasons.push('recitation_audio_missing');
    if(!record?.recitation?.source||!sourcePresent(record.recitation.source))reasons.push('recitation_source_missing');
    if(record?.recitation?.verification_status!=='production_ready')reasons.push('recitation_not_production_ready');
    if(record?.recitation?.rights_status!=='cleared')reasons.push('recitation_rights_not_cleared');
    if(!record?.recitation?.source?.license)reasons.push('recitation_license_missing');
    if(!record?.recitation?.source?.rights_reference)reasons.push('recitation_rights_reference_missing');
    return {ready:reasons.length===0,reasons};
  }
  function learningNoteGate(record){
    const reasons=[];
    if(!record?.learning_note?.text)reasons.push('learning_note_missing');
    if(!sourcePresent(record?.learning_note?.source))reasons.push('learning_note_source_missing');
    if(record?.learning_note?.verification_status!=='production_ready')reasons.push('learning_note_not_production_ready');
    return {ready:reasons.length===0,reasons};
  }

  function register(raw){
    const normalized=normalizeAyah(raw);
    store[key(normalized.surah_id,normalized.ayah_number)]=normalized;
    return clone(normalized);
  }
  function internalGet(id,n){
    if(!validAyah(id,Number(n)))return null;
    const rec=store[key(id,n)];
    return rec?clone(rec):null;
  }
  function productionGet(id,n){
    const rec=internalGet(id,n);
    if(!rec)return null;
    const result=sourceGate(rec);
    if(!result.ready)return null;
    const safe=clone(rec);
    if(!recitationGate(rec).ready)safe.recitation=blankAyah(id,n).recitation;
    if(!learningNoteGate(rec).ready)safe.learning_note=blankAyah(id,n).learning_note;
    return safe;
  }
  function productionSurah(id){
    if(!validSurah(id))return [];
    const out=[];
    for(let n=1;n<=ayahCount(id);n++){
      const rec=productionGet(id,n);
      if(rec)out.push(rec);
    }
    return out;
  }
  function hasPayload(rec){
    return !!(
      rec?.arabic?.text||
      rec?.translation?.text||
      rec?.recitation?.audio_url||
      rec?.learning_note?.text||
      sourcePresent(rec?.arabic?.source)||
      sourcePresent(rec?.translation?.source)||
      sourcePresent(rec?.recitation?.source)||
      sourcePresent(rec?.learning_note?.source)
    );
  }
  function readiness(id,n){
    const rec=internalGet(id,n);
    return sourceGate(rec);
  }
  function expertReadiness(id,n){
    const rec=internalGet(id,n);
    return expertReleaseGate(rec);
  }
  function audioReadiness(id,n){
    const rec=internalGet(id,n);
    const source=sourceGate(rec);
    if(!source.ready)return {ready:false,reasons:[...source.reasons]};
    return recitationGate(rec);
  }
  function learningNoteReadiness(id,n){
    const rec=internalGet(id,n);
    const source=sourceGate(rec);
    if(!source.ready)return {ready:false,reasons:[...source.reasons]};
    return learningNoteGate(rec);
  }
  function inventory(){
    const all=[];
    for(const id of Object.keys(SURAH_META)){
      for(let n=1;n<=ayahCount(id);n++){
        const rec=internalGet(id,n);
        const source=sourceGate(rec);
        const expert=expertReleaseGate(rec);
        all.push({
          surah_id:id,
          ayah_number:n,
          exists:hasPayload(rec),
          source_ready:source.ready,
          production_ready:source.ready,
          expert_release_ready:expert.ready,
          verification_status:rec?.verification?.verification_status||'missing',
          religious_release_ready:rec?.verification?.religious_release_ready===true,
          reasons:[...source.reasons],
          expert_reasons:[...expert.reasons]
        });
      }
    }
    return all;
  }
  function summary(){
    const inv=inventory();
    return {
      schema_version:SCHEMA_VERSION,
      total_slots:inv.length,
      registered:inv.filter(x=>x.exists).length,
      production_ready:inv.filter(x=>x.source_ready).length,
      source_ready:inv.filter(x=>x.source_ready).length,
      expert_release_ready:inv.filter(x=>x.expert_release_ready).length,
      blocked:inv.filter(x=>!x.source_ready).length
    };
  }

  // Pre-create no user-facing text. The six-surah pack starts entirely blocked.
  for(const id of Object.keys(SURAH_META)){
    for(let n=1;n<=ayahCount(id);n++){
      store[key(id,n)]=blankAyah(id,n);
    }
  }

  const api={
    schemaVersion:SCHEMA_VERSION,
    statuses:[...STATUS],
    surahs:clone(SURAH_META),
    register,
    internalGet,
    getAyah:productionGet,
    getSurah:productionSurah,
    readiness,
    expertReadiness,
    audioReadiness,
    learningNoteReadiness,
    inventory,
    summary,
    canRender:(id,n)=>readiness(id,n).ready,
    assertProductionReady:(id,n)=>{
      const r=expertReadiness(id,n);
      if(!r.ready)throw new Error('Kur’an içeriği uzman release onaylı değil: '+r.reasons.join(', '));
      return true;
    }
  };

  window.DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT=api;
  window.DEEN_QURAN=window.DEEN_QURAN||{};
  window.DEEN_QURAN.content=api;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.getVerifiedAyah=productionGet;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.getVerifiedSurah=productionSurah;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.contentReadiness=readiness;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.expertReleaseReadiness=expertReadiness;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.audioReadiness=audioReadiness;

  // User-facing guard: content renderers use source-verified Arabic + translation; expert release certification is tracked separately.
  window.DEEN_QURAN.renderVerifiedAyah=function(id,n,renderer){
    const record=productionGet(id,n);
    if(!record)return false;
    if(typeof renderer!=='function')return false;
    renderer(clone(record));
    return true;
  };

  document.documentElement.dataset.deenQuranContent='v10920';
})();
</script>

<style id="deen-v91206-verse-translation-css">
.deen-ayah-translation{margin-top:10px;padding:13px 14px;border:1px solid rgba(141,205,173,.25);border-left:3px solid #83dcbd;border-radius:12px;background:linear-gradient(125deg,#17403e,#0c3036);direction:ltr;text-align:left;color:#eafaf3}
.deen-ayah-translation strong{display:block;margin-bottom:6px;color:#a4efd3;font:850 10px/1.4 Inter,system-ui,sans-serif}

<script id="deen-v91206-verse-translation-js">
(()=>{
 if(window.DEEN_QURAN_LEARNING_TRANSLATION)return;
 const escapeHtml=x=>String(x??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
 const locale=()=>window.DEEN_APP_LOCALE?.get?.()||'tr';
 function get(rec){const lang=locale(),m=lang==='en'?rec?.translations?.en:lang==='tr'?rec?.translation:null;
  return m&&String(m.language||'').split('-')[0]===lang&&m.verification_status==='production_ready'&&m.source?.provider&&typeof m.text==='string'&&m.text.trim()?m:null;
 }
 const sourceName=(m,lang)=>lang==='en'?'Marmaduke Pickthall · 1930':String(m.source?.edition||m.source?.provider||'');
 function html(rec){
  const lang=locale(),meal=get(rec),label=({tr:'TÜRKÇE MEAL',en:'ENGLISH MEANING',ar:'معنى الآية',de:'ÜBERSETZUNG',es:'TRADUCCIÓN',fr:'TRADUCTION'}[lang]||'MEANING');
  if(!meal)return'<div class="deen-ayah-translation deen-ayah-translation-pending" data-verse-translation="missing" lang="'+lang+'" dir="'+(lang==='ar'?'rtl':'ltr')+'" role="status"><strong>'+label+'</strong><p>'+({tr:'Bu ayetin doğrulanmış Türkçe meali henüz hazır değil.',en:'No source-verified English meaning yet.',ar:'لا تتوفر ترجمة موثقة لهذه الآية بعد.',de:'Für diesen Vers ist noch keine geprüfte Übersetzung verfügbar.',es:'Aún no hay traducción verificada para esta aleya.',fr:'Aucune traduction vérifiée pour ce verset.'}[lang]||'Translation pending.')+'</p></div>';
  const url=String(meal.source?.url||'');
  const link=/^https:\/\//i.test(url)?' · <a href="'+escapeHtml(url)+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">'+(lang==='en'?'Source':'Kaynak')+' ↗</a>':'';
  const note=lang==='en'&&meal.expert_review_status!=='approved'?' · Scholarly review pending':'';
  return'<div class="deen-ayah-translation" data-verse-translation="'+lang+'" lang="'+lang+'" dir="ltr"><strong>'+label+'</strong><p>'+escapeHtml(meal.text)+'</p><small>'+escapeHtml(sourceName(meal,lang)+note)+link+'</small></div>';
 }
 function sourceLine(rec){
  const lang=locale(),m=get(rec);
  if(lang==='ar')return 'النص القرآني: '+String(rec?.arabic?.source?.provider||'—')+' · التفسير العربي الموثّق قيد الإعداد';
  return(lang==='en'?'Arabic: ':'Arapça: ')+String(rec?.arabic?.source?.provider||'—')+
   (lang==='en'?' · Translation: ':' · Meal: ')+(m?sourceName(m,lang):(lang==='en'?'English meaning unavailable':'Türkçe meal bekleniyor'));
 }
 window.DEEN_QURAN_LEARNING_TRANSLATION={get,html,sourceLine,locale,version:'9314arquransourceline1'};
})();
</script>
<script id="deen-v10921-ikhlas-learning-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10921IkhlasLearning)return;
  window.__deenV10921IkhlasLearning=true;

  const VERIFIED_AT='2026-10-04T00:15:00+03:00';
  const SOURCE_REVIEW='DEEN source cross-check: Quran.com + Vikikaynak';
  const ARABIC_SOURCE={
    provider:'Quran.com',
    edition:'Uthmani display',
    reference:'Surah 112',
    url:'https://quran.com/tr/ihlas'
  };
  const MEAL_SOURCE={
    provider:'Vikikaynak',
    edition:'Muhammed Hamdi Yazır, 1938',
    reference:"Kur'an/İhlâs Suresi",
    url:"https://tr.wikisource.org/wiki/Kur%27an/%C4%B0hl%C3%A2s_Suresi"
  };
  const CONTENT=[
    {n:1,arabic:'قُلْ هُوَ ٱللَّهُ أَحَدٌ',meal:'De, o: Allah tek bir tektir.'},
    {n:2,arabic:'ٱللَّهُ ٱلصَّمَدُ',meal:'Allah, o eksiksiz sameddir.'},
    {n:3,arabic:'لَمْ يَلِدْ وَلَمْ يُولَدْ',meal:'Doğurmadı ve doğurulmadı.'},
    {n:4,arabic:'وَلَمْ يَكُن لَّهُۥ كُفُوًا أَحَدٌۢ',meal:'Ona bir küfüv de olmadı.'}
  ];
  const STEPS=['listen','read','meaning','recall','complete'];
  const STEP_LABELS={listen:'Dinle',read:'Oku',meaning:'Anlam',recall:'Hatırla',complete:'Tamamla'};
  let session={ayah:1,step:0,revealed:false};

  const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));
  const api=()=>window.DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT;

  function registerContent(){
    if(!api()?.register)return false;
    for(const item of CONTENT){
      const existing=api()?.internalGet?.('ikhlas',item.n);
      const existingVerification=existing?.verification||null;
      const expertManaged=!!existingVerification&&(
        existingVerification.verification_status==='needs_review'||
        (existingVerification.verified_by&&existingVerification.verified_by!==SOURCE_REVIEW)
      );
      api().register({
        surah_id:'ikhlas',
        ayah_number:item.n,
        arabic:{
          text:item.arabic,
          source:{...ARABIC_SOURCE,reference:'112:'+item.n},
          verification_status:'production_ready'
        },
        translation:{
          language:'tr',
          text:item.meal,
          source:{...MEAL_SOURCE,reference:"İhlâs "+item.n+". ayet"},
          verification_status:'production_ready'
        },
        recitation:existing?.recitation?.audio_url?existing.recitation:{
          audio_url:null,
          qari:null,
          riwayah:null,
          source:{},
          verification_status:'needs_review',
          rights_status:'unknown'
        },
        learning_note:existing?.learning_note?.text?existing.learning_note:{
          text:null,
          source:{},
          verification_status:'needs_review'
        },
        verification:expertManaged?existingVerification:{
          verification_status:'production_ready',
          religious_release_ready:true,
          verified_by:SOURCE_REVIEW,
          verified_at:VERIFIED_AT,
          review_notes:[
            'Arabic verse display cross-checked against Quran.com Surah 112.',
            'Turkish translation uses public-domain Muhammed Hamdi Yazır text from Vikikaynak.',
            'No tafsir or interpretive learning note is released in this stage.'
          ]
        },
        updated_at:expertManaged?(existing?.updated_at||VERIFIED_AT):VERIFIED_AT
      });
    }
    return true;
  }
  function getAyah(n){
    return api()?.getAyah?.('ikhlas',Number(n))||null;
  }
  function ensureSession(){
    const q=DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState?.()||state.quranJourney;
    const current=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Number(q?.profile?.currentAyah||q?.progress?.surahs?.ikhlas?.currentAyah||1)));
    if(!session||session.ayah<1||session.ayah>4)session={ayah:current,step:0,revealed:false};
    return q;
  }
  function sourceLine(r){return window.DEEN_QURAN_LEARNING_TRANSLATION?.sourceLine?.(r)||''}
  const translated=r=>window.DEEN_QURAN_LEARNING_TRANSLATION?.html?.(r)||'';
  function stepDots(){
    return STEPS.map((_,i)=>'<i class="deen-ikhlas-stepdot'+(i<=session.step?' on':'')+'"></i>').join('');
  }
  function currentRecord(){
    return getAyah(session.ayah);
  }
  function blockedHtml(n){
    const readiness=api()?.readiness?.('ikhlas',n);
    return '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Bu ayet production doğrulama kapısından geçmedi. İçerik kullanıcıya gösterilmedi.'+
      (readiness?.reasons?.length?'<br><small>'+esc(readiness.reasons.join(' · '))+'</small>':'')+
      '</div>';
  }
  function cardHtml(){
    const record=currentRecord();
    if(!record)return blockedHtml(session.ayah);
    const stage=STEPS[session.step];
    const title=STEP_LABELS[stage];
    let body='';

    if(stage==='listen'){
      body=
        '<div class="deen-ikhlas-card">'+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-kicker">1 · DİNLE</div>'+
          '<h2>Ayet '+session.ayah+' / 4</h2>'+
          '<p>Önce ayetin sesini dinlemek ideal akıştır. Kıraat kaydı 8/14 ses sistemi aşamasında bağlanacak.</p>'+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-listen"><i>▶</i><span><b>Kıraat henüz bağlı değil</b><small>Yanlış veya kaynaksız ses oynatmak yerine bu adımı güvenli biçimde pasif tutuyoruz.</small></span></div>'+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-arabic" lang="ar">'+esc(record.arabic.text)+'</div>'+translated(record)+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-source">'+esc(sourceLine(record))+'</div>'+
        '</div>';
    }else if(stage==='read'){
      body=
        '<div class="deen-ikhlas-card">'+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-kicker">2 · OKU</div>'+
          '<h2>Metni gözünle takip et</h2>'+
          '<p>Ayetin tamamını yavaşça oku. Bu turda telaffuza otomatik doğru/yanlış hükmü vermiyoruz.</p>'+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-arabic" lang="ar">'+esc(record.arabic.text)+'</div>'+translated(record)+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-source">'+esc(sourceLine(record))+'</div>'+
        '</div>';
    }else if(stage==='meaning'){
      body=
        '<div class="deen-ikhlas-card">'+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-kicker">3 · ANLAM</div>'+
          '<h2>Ayetin mealini bağla</h2>'+
          '<p>Bu aşamada yalnız kaynaklı meal gösterilir; yorum veya tefsir eklemiyoruz.</p>'+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-arabic" lang="ar">'+esc(record.arabic.text)+'</div>'+translated(record)+
          
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-source">'+esc(sourceLine(record))+'</div>'+
        '</div>';
    }else if(stage==='recall'){
      body=
        '<div class="deen-ikhlas-card">'+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-kicker">4 · HATIRLA</div>'+
          '<h2>Metne bakmadan getir</h2>'+
          '<p>Önce ayeti zihninden söylemeye çalış. Sonra kontrol etmek için metni aç.</p>'+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-recall">'+
            '<div class="prompt">İhlâs '+session.ayah+'. ayeti hatırla</div>'+
            (session.revealed
              ?'<div class="deen-ikhlas-arabic" lang="ar">'+esc(record.arabic.text)+'</div>'+translated(record)
              :'<div class="hidden">••• ••• •••</div>')+
          '</div>'+
          (!session.revealed?'<button class="deen-ikhlas-btn secondary" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_IKHLAS.reveal()">AYETİ GÖSTER</button>':'')+
        '</div>';
    }else{
      body=
        '<div class="deen-ikhlas-card">'+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-kicker">5 · TAMAMLA</div>'+
          '<h2>Ayet turunu bitir</h2>'+
          '<p>Bu ayeti ilk öğrenme turunda tamamladığını işaretle. Ezber seviyesi daha sonra tekrarlarla güçlenecek.</p>'+
          '<div class="deen-ikhlas-arabic" lang="ar">'+esc(record.arabic.text)+'</div>'+translated(record)+
          
        '</div>';
    }
    return body;
  }
  function bodyHtml(){
    return '<div class="deen-ikhlas-session">'+
      '<div class="deen-ikhlas-progresshead"><b>İhlâs · Ayet '+session.ayah+'/4</b><span>'+esc(STEP_LABELS[STEPS[session.step]])+'</span></div>'+
      '<div class="deen-ikhlas-steps">'+stepDots()+'</div>'+
      cardHtml()+
      '<div class="deen-ikhlas-actions">'+
        (session.step<STEPS.length-1
          ?'<button class="deen-ikhlas-btn" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_IKHLAS.next()">DEVAM ET</button>'
          :'<button class="deen-ikhlas-btn" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_IKHLAS.finishAyah()">AYETİ TAMAMLA</button>')+
        (session.step>0?'<button class="deen-ikhlas-btn secondary" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_IKHLAS.back()">GERİ</button>':'')+
      '</div>'+
    '</div>';
  }
  function redraw(){
    const mount=document.querySelector('.deen-ikhlas-session');
    if(mount){
      const wrapper=document.createElement('div');
      wrapper.innerHTML=bodyHtml();
      mount.replaceWith(wrapper.firstElementChild);
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function open(ayah){
    registerContent();
    const q=DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState?.()||state.quranJourney;
    const selected=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Number(ayah||q?.profile?.currentAyah||q?.progress?.surahs?.ikhlas?.currentAyah||1)));
    session={ayah:selected,step:0,revealed:false};
    try{DEEN_QURAN?.startSurah?.('ikhlas',selected,'learn')}catch(_){}
    sheet('İhlâs Suresi',bodyHtml());
    document.documentElement.dataset.deenIkhlasLearning='v10921';
    return true;
  }
  function next(){
    if(session.step<STEPS.length-1){
      session.step++;
      session.revealed=false;
      redraw();
    }
    return session.step;
  }
  function back(){
    if(session.step>0){
      session.step--;
      session.revealed=false;
      redraw();
    }
    return session.step;
  }
  function reveal(){
    session.revealed=true;
    redraw();
    return true;
  }
  function finishAyah(){
    const q=DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState?.()||state.quranJourney;
    const s=q.progress.surahs.ikhlas;
    const prior=s.ayahs[session.ayah]||{};
    const firstCompletion=!prior.completedAt;
    const nextState={
      state:prior.state==='solid'?'solid':'strengthening',
      completedAt:prior.completedAt||new Date().toISOString(),
      memorizationLevel:Math.max(1,Number(prior.memorizationLevel)||0),
      repetitions:(Number(prior.repetitions)||0)+1,
      lastStudiedAt:new Date().toISOString()
    };
    DEEN_QURAN.updateAyah('ikhlas',session.ayah,nextState);
    const updated=DEEN_QURAN.ensureState();
    if(firstCompletion)updated.daily.ayahs=(Number(updated.daily.ayahs)||0)+1;

    if(session.ayah<4){
      const nextAyah=session.ayah+1;
      updated.profile.currentSurah='ikhlas';
      updated.profile.currentAyah=nextAyah;
      updated.progress.surahs.ikhlas.currentAyah=nextAyah;
      updated.profile.lastSession={surahId:'ikhlas',ayah:nextAyah,mode:'learn',at:new Date().toISOString()};
      updated.profile.lastSessionAt=updated.profile.lastSession.at;
      DEEN_QURAN.persistState();
      session={ayah:nextAyah,step:0,revealed:false};
      redraw();
      return {done:false,nextAyah};
    }

    updated.profile.currentSurah='ikhlas';
    updated.profile.currentAyah=4;
    updated.progress.surahs.ikhlas.currentAyah=4;
    updated.progress.surahs.ikhlas.status='learning';
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState();

    const mount=document.querySelector('.deen-ikhlas-session');
    if(mount){
      mount.innerHTML=
        '<div class="deen-ikhlas-complete">'+
          '<strong>İlk öğrenme turu tamamlandı</strong>'+
          '<span>İhlâs’ın 4 ayetini Dinle → Oku → Anlam → Hatırla → Tamamla akışıyla bitirdin. Bu henüz “ezberlendi” sayılmaz; sonraki ezber ve tekrar motorları hafızayı güçlendirecek.</span>'+
        '</div>'+
        '<button class="deen-ikhlas-btn" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_IKHLAS.restart()">BAŞTAN GÖZDEN GEÇİR</button>';
    }
    return {done:true,nextAyah:null};
  }
  function restart(){
    session={ayah:1,step:0,revealed:false};
    const q=DEEN_QURAN.ensureState();
    q.profile.currentSurah='ikhlas';
    q.profile.currentAyah=1;
    q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.currentAyah=1;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState();
    redraw();
    return true;
  }
  function model(){
    const record=currentRecord();
    return {
      surah:'ikhlas',
      ayah:session.ayah,
      step:STEPS[session.step],
      stepIndex:session.step,
      revealed:session.revealed,
      contentReady:!!record,
      arabic:record?.arabic?.text||null,
      meal:record?.translation?.text||null
    };
  }

  registerContent();
  ensureSession();

  const previousOpen=typeof DEEN_QURAN?.openSurah==='function'?DEEN_QURAN.openSurah.bind(DEEN_QURAN):null;
  window.DEEN_QURAN=window.DEEN_QURAN||{};
  window.DEEN_QURAN.openSurah=function(id){
    if(id==='ikhlas')return open();
    return previousOpen?previousOpen(id):false;
  };
  window.DEEN_QURAN.openIkhlas=open;

  window.DEEN_QURAN_IKHLAS={
    open,next,back,reveal,finishAyah,restart,model,
    content:()=>CONTENT.map(x=>({...x})),
    check:()=>({
      productionReady:CONTENT.every(x=>!!getAyah(x.n)),
      ayahs:CONTENT.map(x=>({n:x.n,ready:!!getAyah(x.n)})),
      model:model()
    })
  };
})();
</script>

  color:#effcf8;font:850 10px/1.2 var(--deen-font-ui,Inter,system-ui,sans-serif)
}
.deen-mem-btn.secondary{
  border-color:rgba(88,139,142,.2);background:#0a2931;color:#91b1ad
}
.deen-mem-btn.full{grid-column:1/-1}
.deen-mem-overview{
  display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:6px
}
.deen-mem-overview div{
  padding:10px 5px;border:1px solid rgba(82,137,140,.17);
  border-radius:12px;background:#09242c;text-align:center
}
.deen-mem-overview strong{display:block;color:#e7f7f3;font-size:14px}
.deen-mem-overview small{
  display:block;margin-top:4px;color:#6e8c8b;
  font-size:7px;line-height:1.15
}
.deen-mem-done{
  padding:17px;border:1px solid rgba(190,159,80,.2);border-radius:18px;
  background:linear-gradient(180deg,#133536,#0a272e);text-align:center
}
.deen-mem-done strong{display:block;color:#e9dec0;font-size:19px}

<script id="deen-v10922-ikhlas-memorization-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10922IkhlasMemorization)return;
  window.__deenV10922IkhlasMemorization=true;

  const MAX_LEVEL=5;
  const LEVELS={
    1:{title:'Tam metin',desc:'Metnin tamamını görerek oku ve zihninde sesini kur.'},
    2:{title:'Bazı kelimeler gizli',desc:'Yaklaşık üç kelimeden biri gizli. Eksik parçaları zihninden tamamla.'},
    3:{title:'Çoğu kelime gizli',desc:'Yalnız birkaç güçlü ipucu görünür; geri kalanını hatırla.'},
    4:{title:'Başlangıç ipucu',desc:'Sadece ilk kelime görünür. Ayetin devamını tamamen sen getir.'},
    5:{title:'Tamamen ezberden',desc:'Hiçbir metin ipucu yok. Ayeti baştan sona ezberden söyle.'}
  };
  let session={ayah:1,targetLevel:1,revealed:false};

  const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));
  const qstate=()=>DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState?.()||state.quranJourney;
  const content=n=>DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT?.getAyah?.('ikhlas',Number(n))||null;

  function ayahState(n){
    const q=qstate();
    return q?.progress?.surahs?.ikhlas?.ayahs?.[n]||{};
  }
  function learned(n){
    return !!ayahState(n)?.completedAt;
  }
  function currentLevel(n){
    return Math.max(0,Math.min(MAX_LEVEL,Number(ayahState(n)?.memorizationLevel)||0));
  }
  function firstEligible(){
    for(let n=1;n<=4;n++){
      if(learned(n)&&currentLevel(n)<MAX_LEVEL)return n;
    }
    for(let n=1;n<=4;n++){
      if(learned(n))return n;
    }
    return 1;
  }
  function targetFor(n){
    return Math.max(1,Math.min(MAX_LEVEL,currentLevel(n)+1));
  }
  function words(text){
    return String(text||'').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  }
  function maskedTokens(record,level){
    const ws=words(record?.arabic?.text);
    if(level===1)return ws.map(w=>'<span>'+esc(w)+'</span>').join(' ');
    if(level===5)return '';
    return ws.map((w,i)=>{
      let visible=false;
      if(level===2)visible=(i%3)!==1;
      else if(level===3)visible=(i===0||i===ws.length-1);
      else if(level===4)visible=i===0;
      return visible
        ?'<span>'+esc(w)+'</span>'
        :'<span class="deen-mem-token hidden">•••</span>';
    }).join(' ');
  }
  function overview(){
    const q=qstate();
    const rows=[];
    for(let n=1;n<=4;n++){
      const a=q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.ayahs[n];
      rows.push({
        ayah:n,
        learned:!!a.completedAt,
        level:Math.max(0,Math.min(MAX_LEVEL,Number(a.memorizationLevel)||0)),
        correct:Number(a.correctRecalls)||0,
        wrong:Number(a.wrongRecalls)||0,
        solid:a.state==='solid'
      });
    }
    return rows;
  }
  function allMemorized(){
    return overview().every(x=>x.level>=MAX_LEVEL&&x.solid);
  }
  function levelBars(level){
    return Array.from({length:MAX_LEVEL},(_,i)=>
      '<i class="deen-mem-level'+(i<level?' on':'')+'"></i>'
    ).join('');
  }
  function blockedHtml(){
    const anyLearned=overview().some(x=>x.learned);
    if(!anyLearned){
      return '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Ezber motorunu açmadan önce İhlâs’ın öğrenme turunda en az bir ayeti tamamla.</div>'+
        '<button class="deen-mem-btn full" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.openIkhlas(1)">ÖĞRENME TURUNU AÇ</button>';
    }
    return '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Bu ayet öğrenme turunda henüz tamamlanmadı. Önce ayeti öğren, sonra ezber kademesine geç.</div>';
  }
  function exerciseHtml(){
    const record=content(session.ayah);
    if(!record)return '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Doğrulanmamış içerik ezber ekranında gösterilmedi.</div>';
    if(!learned(session.ayah))return blockedHtml();

    const level=session.targetLevel;
    const info=LEVELS[level];
    const visible=session.revealed?record.arabic.text:null;
    let main='';
    if(session.revealed){
      main='<div class="deen-mem-arabic" lang="ar">'+esc(visible)+'</div>'+(window.DEEN_QURAN_LEARNING_TRANSLATION?.html?.(record)||'');
    }else if(level===5){
      main='<div class="deen-mem-empty"><strong>Metin gizli</strong><span>Ayetin tamamını ezberden söyle. Kontrol etmek istersen “Metni göster”e dokun.</span></div>';
    }else{
      main='<div class="deen-mem-arabic" lang="ar">'+maskedTokens(record,level)+'</div>';
    }

    return '<div class="deen-mem-card">'+
      '<div class="deen-mem-kicker">EZBER KADEMESİ '+level+' / '+MAX_LEVEL+'</div>'+
      '<h2>'+esc(info.title)+'</h2>'+
      '<p>'+esc(info.desc)+'</p>'+
      main+
      '<div class="deen-mem-selfcheck">Bu sürüm self-check kullanır: uygulama kıraatini otomatik olarak “doğru” ilan etmez. Metni zihninden söyledikten sonra kendin kontrol et.</div>'+
      (!session.revealed
        ?'<button class="deen-mem-btn secondary full" style="margin-top:10px" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_MEM.reveal()">METNİ GÖSTER</button>'
        :'')+
    '</div>';
  }
  function sessionHtml(){
    const rows=overview();
    const mastered=rows.filter(x=>x.level>=MAX_LEVEL&&x.solid).length;
    const totalCorrect=rows.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.correct,0);
    const totalWrong=rows.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.wrong,0);
    return '<div class="deen-mem-session">'+
      '<div class="deen-mem-head">'+
        '<span><b>İhlâs Ezber Motoru</b><small>Ayet '+session.ayah+' / 4 · '+esc(LEVELS[session.targetLevel].title)+'</small></span>'+
        '<span class="deen-mem-badge">'+mastered+'/4 SAĞLAM</span>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-mem-levels">'+levelBars(session.targetLevel)+'</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-mem-overview">'+
        rows.map(x=>'<div><strong>'+x.level+'/5</strong><small>AYET '+x.ayah+'</small></div>').join('')+
      '</div>'+
      exerciseHtml()+
      (learned(session.ayah)
        ?'<div class="deen-mem-actions">'+
          '<button class="deen-mem-btn secondary" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_MEM.retry()">TEKRAR ET</button>'+
          '<button class="deen-mem-btn" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_MEM.remembered()">HATIRLADIM</button>'+
        '</div>'
        :'')+
      '<div class="deen-quran-note">Self-check toplamı: '+totalCorrect+' başarılı hatırlama · '+totalWrong+' tekrar isteği</div>'+
    '</div>';
  }
  function redraw(){
    const mount=document.querySelector('.deen-mem-session');
    if(!mount)return false;
    const holder=document.createElement('div');
    holder.innerHTML=sessionHtml();
    mount.replaceWith(holder.firstElementChild);
    return true;
  }
  function open(ayah){
    const q=qstate();
    const requested=Number(ayah);
    const selected=(requested>=1&&requested<=4)?requested:firstEligible();
    session={
      ayah:selected,
      targetLevel:targetFor(selected),
      revealed:false
    };
    q.profile.currentSurah='ikhlas';
    q.profile.currentAyah=selected;
    q.profile.lastMode='memorize';
    q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.currentAyah=selected;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    sheet('İhlâs Ezberi',sessionHtml());
    document.documentElement.dataset.deenIkhlasMemorization='v10922';
    return true;
  }
  function reveal(){
    session.revealed=true;
    redraw();
    return true;
  }
  function nextPending(after){
    for(let offset=1;offset<=4;offset++){
      const n=((after-1+offset)%4)+1;
      if(learned(n)&&currentLevel(n)<MAX_LEVEL)return n;
    }
    return null;
  }
  function remembered(){
    if(!learned(session.ayah))return false;
    const a=ayahState(session.ayah);
    const target=session.targetLevel;
    const changes={
      memorizationLevel:Math.max(Number(a.memorizationLevel)||0,target),
      correctRecalls:(Number(a.correctRecalls)||0)+1,
      repetitions:(Number(a.repetitions)||0)+1,
      wrongRecalls:Number(a.wrongRecalls)||0,
      state:target>=MAX_LEVEL?'solid':'strengthening',
      lastStudiedAt:new Date().toISOString()
    };
    DEEN_QURAN.updateAyah('ikhlas',session.ayah,changes);
    const q=qstate();

    if(allMemorized()){
      q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.status='memorized';
      q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.memorizedAt=q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.memorizedAt||new Date().toISOString();
      q.profile.currentSurah='ikhlas';
      q.profile.currentAyah=4;
      q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.currentAyah=4;
      DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
      const mount=document.querySelector('.deen-mem-session');
      if(mount){
        mount.innerHTML=
          '<div class="deen-mem-done"><strong>İhlâs ezberi tamamlandı</strong>'+
          '<span>Dört ayetin tamamı 5/5 kademesini geçti. Sure artık ezberlendi statüsünde; 9/14 tekrar motoru geldiğinde hafıza gücü zaman içinde yeniden ölçülecek.</span></div>'+
          '<button class="deen-mem-btn full" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_MEM.open(1)">EZBERİ GÖZDEN GEÇİR</button>';
      }
      return {memorized:true,nextAyah:null};
    }

    const current=currentLevel(session.ayah);
    if(current<MAX_LEVEL){
      session.targetLevel=current+1;
      session.revealed=false;
      redraw();
      return {memorized:false,nextAyah:session.ayah,nextLevel:session.targetLevel};
    }

    const next=nextPending(session.ayah);
    if(next){
      session={ayah:next,targetLevel:targetFor(next),revealed:false};
      q.profile.currentAyah=next;
      q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.currentAyah=next;
      DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
      redraw();
      return {memorized:false,nextAyah:next,nextLevel:session.targetLevel};
    }
    redraw();
    return {memorized:false,nextAyah:null};
  }
  function retry(){
    if(!learned(session.ayah))return false;
    const a=ayahState(session.ayah);
    const current=Math.max(0,Number(a.memorizationLevel)||0);
    const regressed=Math.max(0,current-1);
    DEEN_QURAN.updateAyah('ikhlas',session.ayah,{
      memorizationLevel:regressed,
      wrongRecalls:(Number(a.wrongRecalls)||0)+1,
      repetitions:(Number(a.repetitions)||0)+1,
      state:regressed>=MAX_LEVEL?'solid':'strengthening',
      lastStudiedAt:new Date().toISOString()
    });
    session.targetLevel=Math.max(1,regressed+1);
    session.revealed=false;
    redraw();
    return {level:regressed,targetLevel:session.targetLevel};
  }
  function model(){
    return {
      ayah:session.ayah,
      targetLevel:session.targetLevel,
      revealed:session.revealed,
      learned:learned(session.ayah),
      currentLevel:currentLevel(session.ayah),
      allMemorized:allMemorized(),
      overview:overview()
    };
  }

  window.DEEN_QURAN=window.DEEN_QURAN||{};
  window.DEEN_QURAN.memorize=open;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.openMemorization=open;
  window.DEEN_QURAN_MEM={open,reveal,remembered,retry,model,overview,allMemorized};
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v10923-quran-interactive-practice-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10923QuranInteractivePractice)return;
  window.__deenV10923QuranInteractivePractice=true;

  const TYPES=['fill_blank','reorder','continuation','word_match','next_chunk'];
  const LABELS={
    fill_blank:'Boşluk',
    reorder:'Sırala',
    continuation:'Devamı',
    word_match:'Eşleştir',
    next_chunk:'Sonraki Parça'
  };
  let session=null;

  const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));
  const qstate=()=>DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState?.()||state.quranJourney;
  const record=n=>DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT?.getAyah?.('ikhlas',Number(n))||null;
  const words=text=>String(text||'').trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
  const uniq=arr=>[...new Set(arr)];
  function shuffle(arr){
    const out=[...arr];
    for(let i=out.length-1;i>0;i--){
      const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));
      [out[i],out[j]]=[out[j],out[i]];
    }
    return out;
  }
  function makeChunks(tokens){
    if(tokens.length<=3)return tokens.map((t,i)=>({id:'c'+i,text:t,index:i}));
    const size=tokens.length>=6?2:1;
    const out=[];
    for(let i=0;i<tokens.length;i+=size){
      out.push({id:'c'+out.length,text:tokens.slice(i,i+size).join(' '),index:out.length});
    }
    return out;
  }
  function ensurePractice(){
    const q=qstate();
    const s=q.progress.surahs.ikhlas;
    s.practice=s.practice&&typeof s.practice==='object'?s.practice:{};
    const p=s.practice;
    p.sessions=Math.max(0,Number(p.sessions)||0);
    p.correct=Math.max(0,Number(p.correct)||0);
    p.wrong=Math.max(0,Number(p.wrong)||0);
    p.byType=p.byType&&typeof p.byType==='object'?p.byType:{};
    for(const type of TYPES){
      p.byType[type]=p.byType[type]&&typeof p.byType[type]==='object'?p.byType[type]:{};
      p.byType[type].correct=Math.max(0,Number(p.byType[type].correct)||0);
      p.byType[type].wrong=Math.max(0,Number(p.byType[type].wrong)||0);
    }
    p.lastType=TYPES.includes(p.lastType)?p.lastType:null;
    p.lastAyah=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Number(p.lastAyah)||1));
    p.lastAt=typeof p.lastAt==='string'?p.lastAt:null;
    return p;
  }
  function eligible(){
    const q=qstate();
    const out=[];
    for(let n=1;n<=4;n++){
      const a=q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.ayahs[n];
      if(a?.completedAt&&record(n))out.push(n);
    }
    return out;
  }
  function defaultAyah(){
    const list=eligible();
    const current=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Number(qstate()?.profile?.currentAyah)||1));
    return list.includes(current)?current:(list[0]||1);
  }
  function otherTokens(exceptAyah){
    const out=[];
    for(let n=1;n<=4;n++){
      if(n===exceptAyah)continue;
      const r=record(n);
      if(r)out.push(...words(r.arabic.text));
    }
    return uniq(out);
  }
  function buildExercise(type,ayah){
    type=TYPES.includes(type)?type:'fill_blank';
    ayah=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Number(ayah)||1));
    const r=record(ayah);
    if(!r)return null;
    const tokens=words(r.arabic.text);
    const chunks=makeChunks(tokens);
    if(type==='fill_blank'){
      const blankIndex=Math.min(tokens.length-1,Math.max(0,(ayah+tokens.length)%tokens.length));
      const answer=tokens[blankIndex];
      const pool=uniq([answer,...otherTokens(ayah)]).filter(Boolean);
      const distractors=pool.filter(x=>x!==answer).slice(0,3);
      return {type,ayah,tokens,blankIndex,answer,options:shuffle([answer,...distractors].map((text,i)=>({id:'f'+i,text})))};
    }
    if(type==='reorder'){
      return {type,ayah,chunks,options:shuffle(chunks.map(x=>({...x})))};
    }
    if(type==='continuation'){
      const prompt=chunks[0];
      const target=chunks[1]||chunks[0];
      const extras=uniq([
        ...chunks.slice(2).map(x=>x.text),
        ...otherTokens(ayah).slice(0,3)
      ]).filter(x=>x!==target.text).slice(0,3);
      return {type,ayah,prompt,target,options:shuffle([{id:target.id,text:target.text},...extras.map((text,i)=>({id:'x'+i,text}))])};
    }
    if(type==='word_match'){
      let pick=uniq(tokens).slice(0,3);
      if(pick.length<2)pick=uniq([...tokens,...otherTokens(ayah)]).slice(0,3);
      const cards=[];
      pick.forEach((text,i)=>{
        cards.push({id:'m'+i+'a',pair:'p'+i,text});
        cards.push({id:'m'+i+'b',pair:'p'+i,text});
      });
      return {type,ayah,cards:shuffle(cards)};
    }
    return {
      type,ayah,chunks,
      start:chunks[0],
      options:shuffle(chunks.slice(1).map(x=>({...x})))
    };
  }
  function newSession(type,ayah){
    const exercise=buildExercise(type,ayah);
    if(!exercise)return null;
    session={
      type:exercise.type,
      ayah:exercise.ayah,
      exercise,
      selected:[],
      matched:[],
      openCards:[],
      cursor:exercise.type==='next_chunk'?1:0,
      done:false,
      feedback:null,
      hadError:false
    };
    return session;
  }
  function sourceLine(r){
    return 'Kaynaklı ayet metni · '+(r?.arabic?.source?.provider||'—')+' · '+(r?.arabic?.source?.reference||'');
  }
  function modeButtons(){
    return TYPES.map(type=>
      '<button class="deen-practice-mode'+(session?.type===type?' active':'')+'" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_PRACTICE.switchType(\''+type+'\')">'+esc(LABELS[type])+'</button>'
    ).join('');
  }
  function chips(options,used=[]){
    return '<div class="deen-practice-chips">'+options.map(o=>
      '<button class="deen-practice-chip'+(used.includes(o.id)?' used':'')+'" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_PRACTICE.choose(\''+esc(o.id)+'\')">'+esc(o.text)+'</button>'
    ).join('')+'</div>';
  }
  function feedbackHtml(){
    if(!session?.feedback)return '';
    return '<div class="deen-practice-feedback '+(session.feedback.good?'good':'bad')+'">'+esc(session.feedback.text)+'</div>';
  }
  function exerciseHtml(){
    if(!session)return '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Pratik oturumu hazırlanamadı.</div>';
    const r=record(session.ayah);
    if(!r)return '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Doğrulanmamış içerik pratik ekranında gösterilmedi.</div>';
    const ex=session.exercise;
    let inner='';

    if(ex.type==='fill_blank'){
      const line=ex.tokens.map((t,i)=>i===ex.blankIndex?'<span class="blank">_____</span>':esc(t)).join(' ');
      inner=
        '<div class="deen-practice-kicker">BOŞLUK DOLDURMA</div>'+
        '<h2>Eksik kelimeyi yerine koy</h2>'+
        '<p>Ayetin eksik parçasını aşağıdaki kelime taşlarından seç.</p>'+
        '<div class="deen-practice-arabic" lang="ar">'+line+'</div>'+
        chips(ex.options);
    }else if(ex.type==='reorder'){
      const selected=session.selected.map(id=>ex.chunks.find(x=>x.id===id)).filter(Boolean);
      inner=
        '<div class="deen-practice-kicker">PARÇALARI SIRALA</div>'+
        '<h2>Ayeti doğru sıraya getir</h2>'+
        '<p>Parçalara sırayla dokun. Son parça yerleşince sistem kontrol eder.</p>'+
        '<div class="deen-practice-answer'+(selected.length?'':' empty')+'">'+
          (selected.length?selected.map(x=>'<span class="deen-practice-chip">'+esc(x.text)+'</span>').join(' '):'Seçtiğin parçalar burada birleşecek')+
        '</div>'+
        chips(ex.options,session.selected);
    }else if(ex.type==='continuation'){
      inner=
        '<div class="deen-practice-kicker">DOĞRU DEVAMI BUL</div>'+
        '<h2>Bu parçadan sonra ne geliyor?</h2>'+
        '<p>Şıklardan harf seçmek yerine doğrudan doğru metin parçasına dokun.</p>'+
        '<div class="deen-practice-arabic" lang="ar">'+esc(ex.prompt.text)+' …</div>'+
        chips(ex.options);
    }else if(ex.type==='word_match'){
      inner=
        '<div class="deen-practice-kicker">KELİME EŞLEŞTİRME</div>'+
        '<h2>Aynı kelimeleri eşleştir</h2>'+
        '<p>Bu aşama anlam testi değildir. Aynı Arapça kelimenin iki kartını bul.</p>'+
        '<div class="deen-practice-cardgrid">'+
          ex.cards.map(card=>{
            const matched=session.matched.includes(card.id);
            const selected=session.openCards.includes(card.id);
            return '<button class="deen-practice-memory'+(matched?' matched':'')+(selected?' selected':'')+'" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_PRACTICE.choose(\''+card.id+'\')">'+esc(card.text)+'</button>';
          }).join('')+
        '</div>';
    }else{
      const built=ex.chunks.slice(0,session.cursor).map(x=>x.text).join(' ');
      const remaining=ex.chunks.slice(session.cursor);
      inner=
        '<div class="deen-practice-kicker">SONRAKİ PARÇA</div>'+
        '<h2>Ayeti adım adım tamamla</h2>'+
        '<p>Görünen kısmın hemen ardından gelen doğru parçayı seç.</p>'+
        '<div class="deen-practice-arabic" lang="ar">'+esc(built)+(remaining.length?' …':'')+'</div>'+
        (remaining.length?chips(shuffle(remaining.map(x=>({...x})))):'');
    }

    return '<div class="deen-practice-card">'+inner+feedbackHtml()+
      '<div class="deen-practice-source">'+esc(sourceLine(r))+'</div>'+
    '</div>';
  }
  function sessionHtml(){
    const p=ensurePractice();
    const list=eligible();
    if(!list.length){
      return '<div class="deen-practice-session">'+
        '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Etkileşimli pratik için önce İhlâs öğrenme turunda en az bir ayeti tamamla.</div>'+
        '<button class="deen-practice-btn full" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN.openIkhlas(1)">ÖĞRENME TURUNU AÇ</button>'+
      '</div>';
    }
    return '<div class="deen-practice-session">'+
      '<div class="deen-practice-head">'+
        '<span><b>İhlâs Pratik Alanı</b><small>Ayet '+session.ayah+' · '+esc(LABELS[session.type])+'</small></span>'+
        '<span class="deen-practice-score">'+p.correct+' doğru · '+p.wrong+' tekrar</span>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-practice-modes">'+modeButtons()+'</div>'+
      exerciseHtml()+
      '<div class="deen-practice-actions">'+
        '<button class="deen-practice-btn secondary" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_PRACTICE.reset()">SIFIRLA</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-practice-btn" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_PRACTICE.nextExercise()">YENİ PRATİK</button>'+
      '</div>'+
    '</div>';
  }
  function redraw(){
    const mount=document.querySelector('.deen-practice-session');
    if(!mount)return false;
    const holder=document.createElement('div');
    holder.innerHTML=sessionHtml();
    mount.replaceWith(holder.firstElementChild);
    return true;
  }
  function mark(correct){
    const q=qstate();
    const p=ensurePractice();
    const bucket=p.byType[session.type];
    const a=q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.ayahs[session.ayah];
    const now=new Date().toISOString();
    if(correct){
      p.correct++;
      bucket.correct++;
      a.correctRecalls=(Number(a.correctRecalls)||0)+1;
    }else{
      p.wrong++;
      bucket.wrong++;
      a.wrongRecalls=(Number(a.wrongRecalls)||0)+1;
      session.hadError=true;
    }
    a.repetitions=(Number(a.repetitions)||0)+1;
    a.lastStudiedAt=now;
    q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.lastStudiedAt=now;
    p.lastAt=now;
    p.lastType=session.type;
    p.lastAyah=session.ayah;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
  }
  function complete(){
    if(session.done)return;
    session.done=true;
    mark(true);
    ensurePractice().sessions++;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    session.feedback={good:true,text:session.hadError?'Tamamlandı. Zorlandığın parçayı bir sonraki turda yeniden güçlendireceğiz.':'Doğru. Bu hatırlama turu tamamlandı.'};
  }
  function fail(message='Henüz değil. Bir kez daha dene.'){
    mark(false);
    session.feedback={good:false,text:message};
  }
  function choose(id){
    if(!session||session.done)return false;
    const ex=session.exercise;

    if(ex.type==='fill_blank'){
      const option=ex.options.find(x=>x.id===id)||ex.options.find(x=>x.text===id);
      if(!option)return false;
      if(option.text===ex.answer){complete()}else fail();
      redraw();
      return session.done;
    }

    if(ex.type==='continuation'){
      const option=ex.options.find(x=>x.id===id);
      if(!option)return false;
      if(option.text===ex.target.text){complete()}else fail();
      redraw();
      return session.done;
    }

    if(ex.type==='reorder'){
      if(session.selected.includes(id))return false;
      session.selected.push(id);
      if(session.selected.length===ex.chunks.length){
        const ok=session.selected.every((cid,i)=>cid===ex.chunks[i].id);
        if(ok)complete();
        else{
          fail('Sıra tam oturmadı. Parçaları temizledim; tekrar kur.');
          session.selected=[];
        }
      }
      redraw();
      return session.done;
    }

    if(ex.type==='word_match'){
      if(session.matched.includes(id)||session.openCards.includes(id))return false;
      session.openCards.push(id);
      if(session.openCards.length===2){
        const [a,b]=session.openCards.map(cid=>ex.cards.find(c=>c.id===cid));
        if(a&&b&&a.pair===b.pair){
          session.matched.push(a.id,b.id);
          session.openCards=[];
          if(session.matched.length===ex.cards.length)complete();
          else session.feedback={good:true,text:'Eşleşti. Kalan kelimeleri bul.'};
        }else{
          fail('Bu iki kart aynı kelime değil. Tekrar dene.');
          session.openCards=[];
        }
      }
      redraw();
      return session.done;
    }

    const target=ex.chunks[session.cursor];
    if(!target)return false;
    if(id===target.id){
      session.cursor++;
      if(session.cursor>=ex.chunks.length)complete();
      else session.feedback={good:true,text:'Doğru parça. Devam et.'};
    }else{
      fail('Bu parça hemen sonra gelmiyor. Tekrar dene.');
    }
    redraw();
    return session.done;
  }
  function open(type,ayah){
    const p=ensurePractice();
    const list=eligible();
    if(!list.length){
      session={type:'fill_blank',ayah:1,exercise:null,selected:[],matched:[],openCards:[],cursor:0,done:false,feedback:null,hadError:false};
      sheet('Kur’an Pratiği',sessionHtml());
      return false;
    }
    const selectedAyah=list.includes(Number(ayah))?Number(ayah):defaultAyah();
    const selectedType=TYPES.includes(type)?type:TYPES[p.sessions%TYPES.length];
    newSession(selectedType,selectedAyah);
    const q=qstate();
    q.profile.currentSurah='ikhlas';
    q.profile.currentAyah=selectedAyah;
    q.profile.lastMode='practice';
    q.progress.surahs.ikhlas.currentAyah=selectedAyah;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    sheet('Kur’an Pratiği',sessionHtml());
    document.documentElement.dataset.deenQuranPractice='v10923';
    return true;
  }
  function switchType(type){
    if(!TYPES.includes(type))return false;
    newSession(type,session?.ayah||defaultAyah());
    redraw();
    return true;
  }
  function reset(){
    if(!session)return false;
    newSession(session.type,session.ayah);
    redraw();
    return true;
  }
  function nextExercise(){
    const p=ensurePractice();
    const currentIndex=TYPES.indexOf(session?.type);
    const nextType=TYPES[(currentIndex+1+TYPES.length)%TYPES.length];
    const list=eligible();
    const currentPos=list.indexOf(session?.ayah);
    const nextAyah=list.length?list[(currentPos+1+list.length)%list.length]:1;
    newSession(nextType,nextAyah);
    p.lastType=nextType;
    p.lastAyah=nextAyah;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    redraw();
    return true;
  }
  function model(){
    const p=ensurePractice();
    return {
      type:session?.type||null,
      ayah:session?.ayah||null,
      done:!!session?.done,
      feedback:session?.feedback?{...session.feedback}:null,
      selected:[...(session?.selected||[])],
      matched:[...(session?.matched||[])],
      cursor:Number(session?.cursor)||0,
      stats:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p))
    };
  }
  function injectDashboard(){
    const quick=document.querySelector('.deen-quran-quick');
    if(!quick||quick.querySelector('[data-quran-practice]'))return false;
    const btn=document.createElement('button');
    btn.type='button';
    btn.setAttribute('data-quran-practice','1');
    btn.innerHTML='<i>◇</i><b>Pratik</b><small>Etkileşimli hatırlama</small>';
    btn.onclick=()=>open();
    quick.appendChild(btn);
    return true;
  }

  window.DEEN_QURAN=window.DEEN_QURAN||{};
  window.DEEN_QURAN.practice=open;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.openPractice=open;
  window.DEEN_QURAN_PRACTICE={
    open,switchType,reset,nextExercise,choose,model,
    buildExercise,
    eligibleAyahs:()=>[...eligible()],
    types:[...TYPES],
    check:()=>({
      eligible:[...eligible()],
      types:[...TYPES],
      model:model()
    })
  };

  const oldDashboard=DEEN_QURAN.dashboardRender||DEEN_QURAN.render;
  if(typeof oldDashboard==='function'&&!oldDashboard.__deenPracticeWrapped){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const out=oldDashboard.apply(this,arguments);
      injectDashboard();
      return out;
    };
    wrapped.__deenPracticeWrapped=true;
    DEEN_QURAN.dashboardRender=wrapped;
    DEEN_QURAN.render=wrapped;
    window.leagueView=wrapped;
    try{leagueView=wrapped}catch(_){}
  }
  document.addEventListener('deen:navigated',()=>{
    if(document.querySelector('.screen.active')?.id==='leagueScreen')setTimeout(injectDashboard,0);
  });
  if(document.querySelector('.screen.active')?.id==='leagueScreen')setTimeout(injectDashboard,0);
})();
</script>


<!-- DEEN v10.9.24 — provider-independent Quran audio engine -->
<style id="deen-v10924-quran-audio-engine-css">

<script id="deen-v10924-quran-audio-engine-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10924QuranAudioEngine)return;
  window.__deenV10924QuranAudioEngine=true;

  let player=null;
  let timer=null;
  let playingKey=null;
  let playIndex=0;
  let status='idle';

  const clamp=(v,min,max)=>Math.min(max,Math.max(min,Number(v)||min));
  const qstate=()=>DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState?.()||state.quranJourney;
  const key=(id,n)=>id+':'+Number(n);
  const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));

  function ensureSettings(){
    const q=qstate();
    q.audio=q.audio&&typeof q.audio==='object'?q.audio:{};
    q.audio.repeatCount=Math.max(1,Math.min(10,Math.round(Number(q.audio.repeatCount)||3)));
    q.audio.gapMs=[0,1000,2000,3000,5000].includes(Number(q.audio.gapMs))?Number(q.audio.gapMs):2000;
    q.audio.loopSelected=q.audio.loopSelected===true;
    q.audio.volume=clamp(q.audio.volume??1,0,1);
    q.audio.lastSurah=typeof q.audio.lastSurah==='string'?q.audio.lastSurah:'ikhlas';
    q.audio.lastAyah=Math.max(1,Number(q.audio.lastAyah)||1);
    return q.audio;
  }
  function verifiedAudio(id,n){
    const rec=DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT?.getAyah?.(id,Number(n));
    if(!rec?.recitation?.audio_url)return null;
    const a=rec.recitation;
    if(a.verification_status!=='production_ready')return null;
    if(a.rights_status!=='cleared')return null;
    if(!a.source?.license||!a.source?.rights_reference)return null;
    return {
      surahId:id,ayah:Number(n),url:a.audio_url,
      qari:a.qari||'Doğrulanmış kıraat',
      riwayah:a.riwayah||null,
      provider:a.source.provider||null,
      license:a.source.license,
      rightsReference:a.source.rights_reference
    };
  }
  function clearTimer(){
    if(timer){clearTimeout(timer);timer=null}
  }
  function makePlayer(){
    if(player)return player;
    const AudioCtor=window.Audio||window.HTMLAudioElement;
    if(!AudioCtor)return null;
    try{
      player=new AudioCtor();
      player.preload='metadata';
      player.addEventListener?.('ended',onEnded);
      player.addEventListener?.('error',onError);
      player.addEventListener?.('play',()=>{status='playing';refreshControls()});
      player.addEventListener?.('pause',()=>{if(status!=='error')status='paused';refreshControls()});
      return player;
    }catch(_){return null}
  }
  function currentInfo(){
    if(!playingKey)return null;
    const [id,n]=playingKey.split(':');
    return verifiedAudio(id,Number(n));
  }
  function stop(reset=true){
    clearTimer();
    const p=makePlayer();
    if(p){
      try{p.pause()}catch(_){}
      if(reset){try{p.currentTime=0}catch(_){}}
    }
    status='idle';
    playingKey=null;
    playIndex=0;
    refreshControls();
    return true;
  }
  async function play(id,n){
    const info=verifiedAudio(id,n);
    if(!info){
      status='unavailable';
      refreshControls();
      return false;
    }
    const p=makePlayer();
    if(!p){status='unsupported';refreshControls();return false}
    clearTimer();
    const k=key(id,n);
    if(playingKey!==k||p.src!==info.url){
      try{p.src=info.url}catch(_){}
      playingKey=k;
      playIndex=0;
    }else if(['done','idle','error','unavailable','unsupported'].includes(status)){
      playIndex=0;
      try{p.currentTime=0}catch(_){}
    }
    const settings=ensureSettings();
    p.volume=settings.volume;
    settings.lastSurah=id;
    settings.lastAyah=Number(n);
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    try{
      const out=p.play();
      if(out&&typeof out.then==='function')await out;
      status='playing';
      refreshControls();
      return true;
    }catch(_){
      status='error';
      refreshControls();
      return false;
    }
  }
  function pause(){
    clearTimer();
    const p=makePlayer();
    try{p?.pause()}catch(_){}
    status='paused';
    refreshControls();
    return true;
  }
  function toggle(id,n){
    const k=key(id,n);
    const p=makePlayer();
    if(playingKey===k&&p&&!p.paused)return pause();
    return play(id,n);
  }
  function onEnded(){
    const info=currentInfo();
    if(!info){stop(false);return}
    const settings=ensureSettings();
    playIndex++;
    if(settings.loopSelected||playIndex<settings.repeatCount){
      status='waiting';
      refreshControls();
      clearTimer();
      timer=setTimeout(()=>{
        const p=makePlayer();
        try{p.currentTime=0}catch(_){}
        play(info.surahId,info.ayah);
      },settings.gapMs);
      return;
    }
    status='done';
    const p=makePlayer();
    try{p.currentTime=0}catch(_){}
    refreshControls();
  }
  function onError(){
    clearTimer();
    status='error';
    refreshControls();
  }
  function setRepeat(v){
    ensureSettings().repeatCount=Math.max(1,Math.min(10,Math.round(Number(v)||1)));
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    refreshControls();
    return ensureSettings().repeatCount;
  }
  function setGap(v){
    const n=Number(v);
    ensureSettings().gapMs=[0,1000,2000,3000,5000].includes(n)?n:2000;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    refreshControls();
    return ensureSettings().gapMs;
  }
  function setLoop(v){
    ensureSettings().loopSelected=v===true;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    refreshControls();
    return ensureSettings().loopSelected;
  }
  function setVolume(v){
    const value=clamp(v,0,1);
    ensureSettings().volume=value;
    const p=makePlayer();
    if(p)p.volume=value;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    return value;
  }
  function statusText(available){
    if(!available)return 'Doğrulanmış ve hakları temizlenmiş kıraat kaydı henüz bağlı değil.';
    if(status==='playing')return 'Kıraat oynatılıyor · '+(playIndex+1)+'. tekrar';
    if(status==='waiting')return 'Sonraki tekrar için bekleniyor…';
    if(status==='paused')return 'Duraklatıldı';
    if(status==='done')return 'Tekrar turu tamamlandı';
    if(status==='error')return 'Ses yüklenemedi. Metin ve öğrenme akışı çalışmaya devam ediyor.';
    if(status==='unsupported')return 'Bu tarayıcı ses oynatmayı desteklemiyor.';
    return 'Kıraat hazır';
  }
  function controlsHtml(id,n){
    const settings=ensureSettings();
    const info=verifiedAudio(id,n);
    const available=!!info;
    const active=playingKey===key(id,n)&&status==='playing';
    return '<div class="deen-qaudio" data-quran-audio="'+esc(key(id,n))+'">'+
      '<div class="deen-qaudio-top">'+
        '<span class="deen-qaudio-title"><b>Ayet kıraati</b><small>'+(available?esc(info.qari):'Ses kaydı bekleniyor')+'</small></span>'+
        '<button class="deen-qaudio-play" type="button" '+(available?'':'disabled')+' onclick="DEEN_QURAN_AUDIO.toggle(\''+esc(id)+'\','+Number(n)+')">'+(active?'Ⅱ':'▶')+'</button>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-qaudio-status">'+esc(statusText(available))+'</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-qaudio-controls">'+
        '<div class="deen-qaudio-control"><label>TEKRAR SAYISI</label><select onchange="DEEN_QURAN_AUDIO.setRepeat(this.value)">'+
          [1,3,5,10].map(v=>'<option value="'+v+'"'+(settings.repeatCount===v?' selected':'')+'>'+v+'×</option>').join('')+
        '</select></div>'+
        '<div class="deen-qaudio-control"><label>AYET ARASI BEKLEME</label><select onchange="DEEN_QURAN_AUDIO.setGap(this.value)">'+
          [[0,'Yok'],[1000,'1 sn'],[2000,'2 sn'],[3000,'3 sn'],[5000,'5 sn']].map(x=>'<option value="'+x[0]+'"'+(settings.gapMs===x[0]?' selected':'')+'>'+x[1]+'</option>').join('')+
        '</select></div>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<button class="deen-qaudio-toggle'+(settings.loopSelected?' on':'')+'" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_AUDIO.setLoop(!DEEN_QURAN_AUDIO.settings().loopSelected)">SEÇİLİ AYETİ LOOP '+(settings.loopSelected?'AÇIK':'KAPALI')+'</button>'+
      '<div class="deen-qaudio-rights">'+(available
        ?'Kaynak: '+esc(info.provider||'—')+' · Lisans: '+esc(info.license)
        :'Ses URL’si production’a ancak kaynak, lisans ve kullanım hakkı kaydı birlikte doğrulanınca açılır.')+'</div>'+
    '</div>';
  }
  function enhanceIkhlas(){
    const model=DEEN_QURAN_IKHLAS?.model?.();
    if(!model||model.step!=='listen')return false;
    const card=document.querySelector('.deen-ikhlas-card');
    if(!card)return false;
    const legacy=card.querySelector('.deen-ikhlas-listen');
    if(legacy)legacy.remove();
    const old=card.querySelector('.deen-qaudio');
    if(old)old.remove();
    const holder=document.createElement('div');
    holder.innerHTML=controlsHtml('ikhlas',model.ayah);
    const arabic=card.querySelector('.deen-ikhlas-arabic');
    if(arabic)arabic.insertAdjacentElement('beforebegin',holder.firstElementChild);
    else card.appendChild(holder.firstElementChild);
    return true;
  }
  function refreshControls(){
    const model=DEEN_QURAN_IKHLAS?.model?.();
    const mount=document.querySelector('.deen-qaudio');
    if(!model||!mount)return false;
    const holder=document.createElement('div');
    holder.innerHTML=controlsHtml('ikhlas',model.ayah);
    mount.replaceWith(holder.firstElementChild);
    return true;
  }
  function wrapIkhlasMethod(name){
    const obj=window.DEEN_QURAN_IKHLAS;
    const base=obj?.[name];
    if(typeof base!=='function'||base.__deenAudioWrapped)return;
    const wrapped=function(){
      if(name==='next'||name==='back'||name==='finishAyah'||name==='restart')stop(false);
      const out=base.apply(this,arguments);
      setTimeout(enhanceIkhlas,0);
      return out;
    };
    wrapped.__deenAudioWrapped=true;
    obj[name]=wrapped;
  }
  function audit(){
    const rows=[];
    for(let n=1;n<=4;n++){
      const rec=DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT?.internalGet?.('ikhlas',n);
      const audio=rec?.recitation||{};
      rows.push({
        ayah:n,
        hasUrl:!!audio.audio_url,
        verification_status:audio.verification_status||'missing',
        rights_status:audio.rights_status||'unknown',
        license:audio.source?.license||null,
        rights_reference:audio.source?.rights_reference||null,
        playable:!!verifiedAudio('ikhlas',n)
      });
    }
    return rows;
  }

  const API={
    play,pause,stop,toggle,
    setRepeat,setGap,setLoop,setVolume,
    settings:()=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(ensureSettings())),
    verifiedAudio,
    controlsHtml,
    enhanceIkhlas,
    audit,
    state:()=>({status,playingKey,playIndex})
  };
  window.DEEN_QURAN_AUDIO=API;
  window.DEEN_QURAN=window.DEEN_QURAN||{};
  window.DEEN_QURAN.audio=API;

  ['open','next','back','reveal','finishAyah','restart'].forEach(wrapIkhlasMethod);
  setTimeout(enhanceIkhlas,0);
  document.documentElement.dataset.deenQuranAudio='v10924';
})();
</script>
