<script id="deen-v10925-quran-srs-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10925QuranSrs)return;
  window.__deenV10925QuranSrs=true;

  const HOUR=60*60*1000;
  const DAY=24*HOUR;
  const OUTCOMES=['again','hard','good','easy'];
  const SURAH_NAMES={fatiha:'Fâtiha',ikhlas:'İhlâs',falaq:'Felak',nas:'Nâs',kawthar:'Kevser',asr:'Asr'};
  let reviewSession=null;

  const qstate=()=>DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState?.()||state.quranJourney;
  const num=(v,f=0)=>Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):f;
  const iso=t=>new Date(t).toISOString();
  const nowMs=()=>Date.now();
  const clone=v=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));
  const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));

  function ensureMeta(id,n){
    const q=qstate();
    const a=q?.progress?.surahs?.[id]?.ayahs?.[n];
    if(!a)return null;
    a.srs=a.srs&&typeof a.srs==='object'?a.srs:{};
    const s=a.srs;
    s.reviewCount=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(s.reviewCount,0)));
    s.successStreak=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(s.successStreak,0)));
    s.lapseCount=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(s.lapseCount,0)));
    s.stabilityDays=Math.max(0,num(s.stabilityDays,0));
    s.lastOutcome=OUTCOMES.includes(s.lastOutcome)?s.lastOutcome:null;
    s.lastReviewAt=typeof s.lastReviewAt==='string'?s.lastReviewAt:null;
    return {ayah:a,srs:s};
  }
  function stateLabel(v){
    return ({new:'New',learning:'Learning',strengthening:'Strengthening',solid:'Solid'})[v]||'New';
  }
  function intervalFor(a,s,outcome){
    const current=a.state||'new';
    const streak=s.successStreak;
    const memory=Math.max(0,Math.min(5,num(a.memorizationLevel,0)));

    if(outcome==='again')return {ms:10*60*1000,nextState:current==='new'?'learning':(current==='solid'?'strengthening':'learning'),stability:.01};
    if(outcome==='hard'){
      const base=current==='solid'?2*DAY:6*HOUR;
      return {
        ms:Math.max(base,Math.min(3*DAY,(s.stabilityDays||.25)*DAY*.55)),
        nextState:current==='new'?'learning':(current==='solid'?'strengthening':current),
        stability:Math.max(.25,(s.stabilityDays||.25)*.7)
      };
    }
    if(outcome==='easy'){
      if(current==='new')return {ms:3*DAY,nextState:'strengthening',stability:3};
      if(current==='learning')return {ms:7*DAY,nextState:'strengthening',stability:7};
      if(current==='strengthening')return {ms:21*DAY,nextState:memory>=4?'solid':'strengthening',stability:21};
      const days=Math.min(90,Math.max(30,(s.stabilityDays||21)*1.9));
      return {ms:days*DAY,nextState:'solid',stability:days};
    }

    if(current==='new')return {ms:DAY,nextState:'learning',stability:1};
    if(current==='learning'){
      const mature=streak>=1;
      return {ms:(mature?3:1)*DAY,nextState:mature?'strengthening':'learning',stability:mature?3:1};
    }
    if(current==='strengthening'){
      const canSolid=memory>=5&&streak>=2;
      const days=canSolid?14:5;
      return {ms:days*DAY,nextState:canSolid?'solid':'strengthening',stability:days};
    }
    const days=Math.min(60,Math.max(14,(s.stabilityDays||14)*1.6));
    return {ms:days*DAY,nextState:'solid',stability:days};
  }
  function updateSurahStatus(id){
    const q=qstate();
    const surah=q.progress.surahs[id];
    if(!surah)return;
    const ayahs=Object.values(surah.ayahs||{});
    const learned=ayahs.filter(a=>!!a.completedAt);
    if(!learned.length)return;
    if(learned.some(a=>a.nextReviewAt&&Date.parse(a.nextReviewAt)<=nowMs())){
      surah.status='review_due';
      q.memorizedSurahs=Array.isArray(q.memorizedSurahs)?q.memorizedSurahs.filter(x=>x!==id):[];
    }else if(learned.length===ayahs.length&&learned.every(a=>a.state==='solid'&&num(a.memorizationLevel,0)>=5)){
      surah.status='memorized';
      surah.memorizedAt=surah.memorizedAt||new Date().toISOString();
    }else{
      surah.status='learning';
      q.memorizedSurahs=Array.isArray(q.memorizedSurahs)?q.memorizedSurahs.filter(x=>x!==id):[];
    }
  }
  function record(id,n,outcome='good',source='review'){
    if(!OUTCOMES.includes(outcome))outcome='good';
    const info=ensureMeta(id,n);
    if(!info)return null;
    const {ayah:a,srs:s}=info;
    if(!a.completedAt)return null;

    const at=nowMs();
    if(outcome==='again'){
      s.successStreak=0;
      s.lapseCount++;
    }else if(outcome==='hard'){
      s.successStreak=Math.max(0,s.successStreak);
    }else{
      s.successStreak++;
    }
    s.reviewCount++;
    s.lastOutcome=outcome;
    s.lastReviewAt=iso(at);

    const plan=intervalFor(a,s,outcome);
    s.stabilityDays=plan.stability;
    a.state=plan.nextState;
    a.lastStudiedAt=iso(at);
    a.nextReviewAt=iso(at+plan.ms);

    const q=qstate();
    q.review.history=Array.isArray(q.review.history)?q.review.history:[];
    q.review.history.push({
      id:id+':'+Number(n)+':'+at,
      surahId:id,
      ayah:Number(n),
      outcome,
      source:String(source||'review'),
      reviewedAt:iso(at),
      dueAt:a.nextReviewAt,
      state:a.state,
      stabilityDays:s.stabilityDays
    });
    if(q.review.history.length>500)q.review.history=q.review.history.slice(-500);

    DEEN_QURAN.scheduleReview(id,n,a.nextReviewAt,'srs:'+outcome);
    q.review.lastRunAt=iso(at);
    updateSurahStatus(id);
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();

    return {
      surahId:id,ayah:Number(n),outcome,
      state:a.state,
      dueAt:a.nextReviewAt,
      intervalMs:plan.ms,
      stabilityDays:s.stabilityDays,
      successStreak:s.successStreak,
      lapseCount:s.lapseCount
    };
  }
  function bootstrap(id,n){
    const info=ensureMeta(id,n);
    if(!info||!info.ayah.completedAt)return null;
    if(info.ayah.nextReviewAt)return null;
    if(info.srs.reviewCount===0&&num(info.ayah.memorizationLevel,0)<5){
      info.ayah.state='new';
    }
    return record(id,n,'good','learning_complete');
  }
  function dueItems(includeToday=true){
    const q=qstate();
    const limit=includeToday?(()=>{const d=new Date();d.setHours(23,59,59,999);return d.getTime()})():nowMs();
    const map=new Map();
    for(const item of q.review.queue||[]){
      const t=Date.parse(item?.dueAt);
      if(!Number.isFinite(t)||t>limit)continue;
      const id=String(item.surahId||'');
      const n=Number(item.ayah);
      const a=q?.progress?.surahs?.[id]?.ayahs?.[n];
      if(!a?.completedAt)continue;
      map.set(id+':'+n,{
        id:id+':'+n,surahId:id,ayah:n,dueAt:item.dueAt,
        state:a.state||'new',
        memorizationLevel:num(a.memorizationLevel,0),
        srs:clone(a.srs||{})
      });
    }
    return [...map.values()].sort((a,b)=>Date.parse(a.dueAt)-Date.parse(b.dueAt));
  }
  function risk(item){
    const overdue=Math.max(0,nowMs()-Date.parse(item.dueAt||0))/DAY;
    const lapses=num(item.srs?.lapseCount,0);
    const stability=Math.max(.1,num(item.srs?.stabilityDays,.1));
    return Math.round((overdue+1)*(1+lapses*.45)*100/stability);
  }
  function nextDue(){
    return dueItems(true).sort((a,b)=>risk(b)-risk(a))[0]||null;
  }
  function timeLabel(isoDate){
    const diff=Date.parse(isoDate)-nowMs();
    if(diff<=0){
      const hours=Math.floor(Math.abs(diff)/HOUR);
      return hours<1?'Şimdi':'Gecikmiş · '+hours+' sa';
    }
    const hours=Math.round(diff/HOUR);
    if(hours<24)return hours+' sa sonra';
    return Math.round(hours/24)+' gün sonra';
  }
  function reviewHtml(){
    const items=dueItems(true);
    if(!items.length){
      return '<div class="deen-srs-session"><div class="deen-srs-empty"><strong>Bugün için tekrar kalmadı</strong>'+
        '<span>Yeni tekrar zamanı ayetlerin forgetting-risk planına göre otomatik oluşacak.</span></div></div>';
    }
    const item=reviewSession?.item||nextDue();
    reviewSession=reviewSession&&reviewSession.item?.id===item.id?reviewSession:{item,revealed:false};
    const rec=DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT?.getAyah?.(item.surahId,item.ayah);
    if(!rec)return '<div class="deen-quran-pending">Doğrulanmamış içerik tekrar ekranında gösterilmedi.</div>';

    return '<div class="deen-srs-session">'+
      '<div class="deen-srs-head">'+
        '<span><b>Kur’an Tekrarı</b><small>'+esc(SURAH_NAMES[item.surahId]||item.surahId)+' · Ayet '+item.ayah+'</small></span>'+
        '<span class="deen-srs-count">'+items.length+' tekrar</span>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-srs-card">'+
        '<div class="deen-srs-kicker">'+esc(stateLabel(item.state).toUpperCase())+' · RİSK '+risk(item)+'</div>'+
        '<h2>Ayetin tamamını hatırla</h2>'+
        '<p>Önce metne bakmadan zihninden getir. Sonra metni aç ve kendi hatırlamanı değerlendir.</p>'+
        (reviewSession.revealed
          ?'<div class="deen-srs-arabic" lang="ar">'+esc(rec.arabic.text)+'</div>'
          :'<div class="deen-srs-prompt"><strong>Metin gizli</strong><span>Hazır olduğunda kontrol için ayeti göster.</span></div>')+
        '<div class="deen-srs-meta">'+
          '<div><strong>'+Math.round(num(item.memorizationLevel,0))+'/5</strong><small>EZBER</small></div>'+
          '<div><strong>'+num(item.srs?.successStreak,0)+'</strong><small>DOĞRU SERİ</small></div>'+
          '<div><strong>'+num(item.srs?.lapseCount,0)+'</strong><small>UNUTMA</small></div>'+
        '</div>'+
      '</div>'+
      (!reviewSession.revealed
        ?'<button class="deen-srs-btn good" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_SRS.reveal()">AYETİ GÖSTER</button>'
        :'<div class="deen-srs-actions">'+
          '<button class="deen-srs-btn again" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_SRS.grade(\'again\')">UNUTTUM</button>'+
          '<button class="deen-srs-btn hard" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_SRS.grade(\'hard\')">ZORLANDIM</button>'+
          '<button class="deen-srs-btn good" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_SRS.grade(\'good\')">HATIRLADIM</button>'+
        '</div>')+
    '</div>';
  }
  function redrawReview(){
    const mount=document.querySelector('.deen-srs-session');
    if(!mount)return false;
    const holder=document.createElement('div');
    holder.innerHTML=reviewHtml();
    mount.replaceWith(holder.firstElementChild);
    return true;
  }
  function openReview(){
    reviewSession=null;
    sheet('Kur’an Tekrarı',reviewHtml());
    document.documentElement.dataset.deenQuranSrs='v10925';
    return true;
  }
  function reveal(){
    if(!reviewSession)return false;
    reviewSession.revealed=true;
    redrawReview();
    return true;
  }
  function grade(outcome){
    if(!reviewSession?.item||!OUTCOMES.includes(outcome))return false;
    const item=reviewSession.item;
    const result=record(item.surahId,item.ayah,outcome,'review_session');
    reviewSession=null;
    redrawReview();
    return result;
  }
  function inspect(id,n){
    const q=qstate();
    const a=q?.progress?.surahs?.[id]?.ayahs?.[n];
    if(!a)return null;
    return {
      surahId:id,ayah:Number(n),
      state:a.state,
      memorizationLevel:num(a.memorizationLevel,0),
      nextReviewAt:a.nextReviewAt||null,
      srs:clone(a.srs||{}),
      queued:(q.review.queue||[]).find(x=>x.surahId===id&&Number(x.ayah)===Number(n))||null
    };
  }
  function audit(){
    const q=qstate(),rows=[];
    for(const [id,surah] of Object.entries(q.progress.surahs||{})){
      for(const [n,a] of Object.entries(surah.ayahs||{})){
        if(!a.completedAt)continue;
        rows.push(inspect(id,Number(n)));
      }
    }
    return rows;
  }
  function bootstrapAll(){
    const q=qstate();
    let count=0;
    for(const [id,surah] of Object.entries(q.progress.surahs||{})){
      for(const [n,a] of Object.entries(surah.ayahs||{})){
        if(a.completedAt&&!a.nextReviewAt){
          if(bootstrap(id,Number(n)))count++;
        }
      }
    }
    return count;
  }
  function wrapResult(obj,name,successOutcome='good',failureOutcome='again'){
    const base=obj?.[name];
    if(typeof base!=='function'||base.__deenSrsWrapped)return;
    const wrapped=function(){
      const before=obj.model?.();
      const out=base.apply(this,arguments);
      const after=obj.model?.();
      const ayah=Number(before?.ayah||after?.ayah);
      if(Number.isFinite(ayah)){
        if(name==='retry')record('ikhlas',ayah,failureOutcome,'memorization');
        else if(name==='remembered')record('ikhlas',ayah,successOutcome,'memorization');
      }
      return out;
    };
    wrapped.__deenSrsWrapped=true;
    obj[name]=wrapped;
  }
  function wrapPractice(){
    const obj=window.DEEN_QURAN_PRACTICE;
    const base=obj?.choose;
    if(typeof base!=='function'||base.__deenSrsWrapped)return;
    const wrapped=function(){
      const before=obj.model?.();
      const out=base.apply(this,arguments);
      const after=obj.model?.();
      const ayah=Number(before?.ayah||after?.ayah);
      if(Number.isFinite(ayah)){
        const wrongDelta=num(after?.stats?.wrong,0)-num(before?.stats?.wrong,0);
        const correctDelta=num(after?.stats?.correct,0)-num(before?.stats?.correct,0);
        if(wrongDelta>0)record('ikhlas',ayah,'again','practice');
        else if(correctDelta>0)record('ikhlas',ayah,'good','practice');
      }
      return out;
    };
    wrapped.__deenSrsWrapped=true;
    obj.choose=wrapped;
  }
  function wrapLearning(){
    const obj=window.DEEN_QURAN_IKHLAS;
    const base=obj?.finishAyah;
    if(typeof base!=='function'||base.__deenSrsWrapped)return;
    const wrapped=function(){
      const before=obj.model?.();
      const out=base.apply(this,arguments);
      const ayah=Number(before?.ayah);
      if(Number.isFinite(ayah))bootstrap('ikhlas',ayah);
      return out;
    };
    wrapped.__deenSrsWrapped=true;
    obj.finishAyah=wrapped;
  }

  window.DEEN_QURAN=window.DEEN_QURAN||{};
  window.DEEN_QURAN.review=openReview;
  window.DEEN_QURAN_SRS={
    record,bootstrap,bootstrapAll,
    dueItems,nextDue,risk,inspect,audit,
    openReview,reveal,grade,
    outcomes:[...OUTCOMES]
  };
  wrapLearning();
  wrapResult(window.DEEN_QURAN_MEM,'remembered','good','again');
  wrapResult(window.DEEN_QURAN_MEM,'retry','good','again');
  wrapPractice();
  bootstrapAll();
  document.documentElement.dataset.deenQuranSrs='v10925';
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v10926-quran-daily-verse-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10926QuranDailyVerse)return;
  window.__deenV10926QuranDailyVerse=true;

  const NOTES={
    'ikhlas:1':{
      text:'Bu ayetin mealinde Allah’ın tekliği vurgulanır.',
      source:{provider:'Vikikaynak',edition:'Muhammed Hamdi Yazır, 1938',reference:'İhlâs 1. ayet',url:"https://tr.wikisource.org/wiki/Kur%27an/%C4%B0hl%C3%A2s_Suresi"},
      question:'Bu ayetin mealinde hangi ana fikir öne çıkıyor?',
      correct:'Allah’ın tek olması',
      options:['Allah’ın tek olması','Bir yolculuk anlatımı','Bir zaman ölçüsü','Bir tabiat olayı']
    },
    'ikhlas:2':{
      text:'Bu ayetin mealinde Allah için “samed” ifadesi yer alır.',
      source:{provider:'Vikikaynak',edition:'Muhammed Hamdi Yazır, 1938',reference:'İhlâs 2. ayet',url:"https://tr.wikisource.org/wiki/Kur%27an/%C4%B0hl%C3%A2s_Suresi"},
      question:'Meal içinde Allah için hangi ifade kullanılıyor?',
      correct:'Samed',
      options:['Samed','Yolcu','Sabah','Yağmur']
    },
    'ikhlas:3':{
      text:'Bu ayet doğurmama ve doğurulmama ifadelerini birlikte verir.',
      source:{provider:'Vikikaynak',edition:'Muhammed Hamdi Yazır, 1938',reference:'İhlâs 3. ayet',url:"https://tr.wikisource.org/wiki/Kur%27an/%C4%B0hl%C3%A2s_Suresi"},
      question:'Bu ayetin mealinde hangi iki ifade birlikte geçiyor?',
      correct:'Doğurmadı ve doğurulmadı',
      options:['Doğurmadı ve doğurulmadı','Geldi ve gitti','Uyudu ve uyandı','Yürüdü ve durdu']
    },
    'ikhlas:4':{
      text:'Bu ayetin mealinde Allah’a hiçbir küfüv olmadığı ifade edilir.',
      source:{provider:'Vikikaynak',edition:'Muhammed Hamdi Yazır, 1938',reference:'İhlâs 4. ayet',url:"https://tr.wikisource.org/wiki/Kur%27an/%C4%B0hl%C3%A2s_Suresi"},
      question:'Bu ayetin mealinde hangi fikir ifade ediliyor?',
      correct:'Ona hiçbir küfüv olmadığı',
      options:['Ona hiçbir küfüv olmadığı','Bir günün uzunluğu','Bir yolun yönü','Bir mevsimin gelişi']
    }
  };

  const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));
  const qstate=()=>DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState?.()||state.quranJourney;
  const todayKey=()=>{
    const d=new Date();
    return d.getFullYear()+'-'+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0');
  };
  const prettyDate=()=>new Date().toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'numeric',month:'short'});
  const stableHash=str=>{
    let h=2166136261;
    for(let i=0;i<str.length;i++){h^=str.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619)}
    return h>>>0;
  };
  function readyPool(){
    const out=[];
    for(let n=1;n<=4;n++){
      const rec=DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT?.getAyah?.('ikhlas',n);
      const note=NOTES['ikhlas:'+n];
      if(rec&&note)out.push({id:'ikhlas:'+n,surahId:'ikhlas',ayah:n,record:rec,note});
    }
    return out;
  }
  function ensureDailyState(){
    const q=qstate();
    q.dailyVerse=q.dailyVerse&&typeof q.dailyVerse==='object'?q.dailyVerse:{};
    const d=q.dailyVerse;
    const date=todayKey();
    if(d.date!==date){
      q.dailyVerse={
        date,
        selectedId:null,
        answered:false,
        correct:null,
        attempts:0,
        answeredAt:null
      };
    }
    return q.dailyVerse;
  }
  function selection(){
    const pool=readyPool();
    if(!pool.length)return null;
    const d=ensureDailyState();
    if(d.selectedId){
      const existing=pool.find(x=>x.id===d.selectedId);
      if(existing)return existing;
    }
    const selected=pool[stableHash(d.date)%pool.length];
    d.selectedId=selected.id;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    return selected;
  }
  function injectVerifiedNote(item){
    const current=DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT?.internalGet?.(item.surahId,item.ayah);
    if(!current)return false;
    const note=item.note;
    DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT.register({
      ...current,
      learning_note:{
        text:note.text,
        source:{...note.source},
        verification_status:'production_ready'
      }
    });
    return !!DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT?.getAyah?.(item.surahId,item.ayah)?.learning_note?.text;
  }
  function verifyAllNotes(){
    const pool=readyPool();
    for(const item of pool)injectVerifiedNote(item);
    return pool.length;
  }
  function itemNow(){
    verifyAllNotes();
    const selected=selection();
    if(!selected)return null;
    const rec=DEEN_QURAN_CONTENT?.getAyah?.(selected.surahId,selected.ayah);
    if(!rec?.learning_note?.text)return null;
    return {...selected,record:rec,note:{...selected.note,text:rec.learning_note.text}};
  }
  function optionClass(text,state){
    if(!state.answered)return '';
    if(text===itemNow()?.note?.correct)return ' correct';
    if(state.correct===false&&text===state.lastChoice)return ' wrong';
    return '';
  }
  function html(){
    const item=itemNow();
    if(!item){
      return '<div class="deen-dailyverse"><div class="deen-quran-pending">Bugünün Ayeti için production-ready içerik ve kaynaklı öğrenme notu bulunamadı.</div></div>';
    }
    const d=ensureDailyState();
    const r=item.record;
    const note=item.note;
    return '<div class="deen-dailyverse">'+
      '<div class="deen-dailyverse-head">'+
        '<span><b>Bugünün Ayeti</b><small>Doğrulanmış günlük öğrenme kartı</small></span>'+
        '<span class="deen-dailyverse-date">'+esc(prettyDate())+'</span>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<section class="deen-dailyverse-card">'+
        '<div class="deen-dailyverse-kicker">İHLÂS · '+item.ayah+'. AYET</div>'+
        '<div class="deen-dailyverse-arabic" lang="ar">'+esc(r.arabic.text)+'</div>'+
        '<div class="deen-dailyverse-meaning"><b>TÜRKÇE MEAL</b><p>'+esc(r.translation.text)+'</p></div>'+
        '<div class="deen-dailyverse-note"><b>KISA ÖĞRENME NOTU</b><p>'+esc(note.text)+'</p></div>'+
        '<div class="deen-dailyverse-source">Arapça: '+esc(r.arabic.source.provider||'—')+' · Meal/Not: Muhammed Hamdi Yazır (1938), Vikikaynak</div>'+
      '</section>'+
      '<section class="deen-dailyverse-question">'+
        '<h3>'+esc(note.question)+'</h3>'+
        '<div class="deen-dailyverse-options">'+
          note.options.map(text=>
            '<button type="button" class="deen-dailyverse-option'+optionClass(text,d)+'" '+(d.answered?'disabled':'')+
            ' onclick="DEEN_QURAN_DAILY.answer(\''+esc(text).replace(/&#39;/g,"\\'")+'\')">'+esc(text)+'</button>'
          ).join('')+
        '</div>'+
        (d.answered
          ?'<div class="deen-dailyverse-result '+(d.correct?'good':'bad')+'">'+
            (d.correct?'Doğru. Meal ve kısa nottaki ana fikri yakaladın.':'Bu turda doğru seçenek: '+esc(note.correct)+'.')+
          '</div>'
          :'')+
      '</section>'+
    '</div>';
  }
  function redraw(){
    const mount=document.querySelector('.deen-dailyverse');
    if(!mount)return false;
    const holder=document.createElement('div');
    holder.innerHTML=html();
    mount.replaceWith(holder.firstElementChild);
    return true;
  }
  function open(){
    const item=itemNow();
    sheet('Bugünün Ayeti',html());
    document.documentElement.dataset.deenQuranDailyVerse='v10926';
    return !!item;
  }
  function answer(choice){
    const item=itemNow();
    if(!item)return false;
    const d=ensureDailyState();
    if(d.answered)return d.correct===true;
    d.attempts=Math.max(0,Number(d.attempts)||0)+1;
    d.lastChoice=String(choice);
    d.correct=String(choice)===item.note.correct;
    d.answered=true;
    d.answeredAt=new Date().toISOString();
    const q=qstate();
    q.dailyVerse=d;
    q.profile.lastMode='daily_verse';
    q.profile.lastSessionAt=d.answeredAt;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    if(window.DEEN_QURAN_SRS?.record){
      DEEN_QURAN_SRS.record(item.surahId,item.ayah,d.correct?'good':'hard','daily_verse');
    }
    redraw();
    return d.correct;
  }
  function model(){
    const item=itemNow();
    const d=ensureDailyState();
    return item?{
      date:d.date,
      selectedId:item.id,
      ayah:item.ayah,
      answered:d.answered,
      correct:d.correct,
      attempts:d.attempts,
      arabic:item.record.arabic.text,
      meal:item.record.translation.text,
      note:item.note.text,
      question:item.note.question,
      answer:item.note.correct,
      ready:true
    }:{date:d.date,ready:false};
  }

  verifyAllNotes();
  window.DEEN_QURAN=window.DEEN_QURAN||{};
  window.DEEN_QURAN.dailyVerse=open;
  window.DEEN_QURAN_DAILY={
    open,answer,model,selection,readyPool:()=>readyPool().map(x=>({id:x.id,ayah:x.ayah}))
  };
})();
</script>

<script id="deen-v10927-quran-word-by-word-js">
(()=>{
  if(window.__deenV10927QuranWordByWord)return;
  window.__deenV10927QuranWordByWord=true;

  const SOURCE={
    morphology:{
      provider:'Quranic Arabic Corpus',
      reference:'Surah 112 word-by-word morphology',
      url:'https://corpus.quran.com/wordbyword.jsp?chapter=112&verse=1'
    },
    context:{
      provider:'Quran.com',
      reference:'Surah 112 Turkish verse context',
      url:'https://quran.com/tr/ihlas'
    }
  };

  const WORDS={
    1:[
      {id:'112:1:1',key:'qul',arabic:'قُلْ',trans:'qul',gloss:'De / söyle',grammar:'Emir fiili'},
      {id:'112:1:2',key:'huwa',arabic:'هُوَ',trans:'huwa',gloss:'O',grammar:'Zamir'},
      {id:'112:1:3',key:'allah',arabic:'ٱللَّهُ',trans:'allāhu',gloss:'Allah',grammar:'Özel isim'},
      {id:'112:1:4',key:'ahad',arabic:'أَحَدٌ',trans:'aḥadun',gloss:'Bir / tek',grammar:'İsim'}
    ],
    2:[
      {id:'112:2:1',key:'allah',arabic:'ٱللَّهُ',trans:'allāhu',gloss:'Allah',grammar:'Özel isim'},
      {id:'112:2:2',key:'samad',arabic:'ٱلصَّمَدُ',trans:'al-ṣamadu',gloss:'Samed',grammar:'İsim'}
    ],
    3:[
      {id:'112:3:1',key:'lam',arabic:'لَمْ',trans:'lam',gloss:'Olumsuzluk: …medi / …madı',grammar:'Olumsuzluk edatı'},
      {id:'112:3:2',key:'yalid',arabic:'يَلِدْ',trans:'yalid',gloss:'Doğurur',grammar:'Fiil'},
      {id:'112:3:3',key:'walam',arabic:'وَلَمْ',trans:'wa-lam',gloss:'Ve …medi / …madı',grammar:'Bağlaç + olumsuzluk'},
      {id:'112:3:4',key:'yulad',arabic:'يُولَدْ',trans:'yūlad',gloss:'Doğurulur',grammar:'Edilgen fiil'}
    ],
    4:[
      {id:'112:4:1',key:'walam',arabic:'وَلَمْ',trans:'wa-lam',gloss:'Ve … değil / olmadı',grammar:'Bağlaç + olumsuzluk'},
      {id:'112:4:2',key:'yakun',arabic:'يَكُن',trans:'yakun',gloss:'Olur / vardır',grammar:'Fiil'},
      {id:'112:4:3',key:'lahu',arabic:'لَّهُۥ',trans:'lahu',gloss:'O’na / O’nun için',grammar:'Edat + zamir'},
      {id:'112:4:4',key:'kufuwan',arabic:'كُفُوًا',trans:'kufuwan',gloss:'Denk / eş',grammar:'İsim'},
      {id:'112:4:5',key:'ahad',arabic:'أَحَدٌۢ',trans:'aḥadun',gloss:'Hiç kimse / biri',grammar:'İsim'}
    ]
  };

  let view={ayah:1,selectedId:null};
  const esc=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,ch=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[ch]));
  const qstate=()=>DEEN_QURAN?.ensureState?.()||state.quranJourney;

  function ensureProgress(){
    const q=qstate();
    q.wordByWord=q.wordByWord&&typeof q.wordByWord==='object'?q.wordByWord:{};
    const w=q.wordByWord;
    w.words=w.words&&typeof w.words==='object'?w.words:{};
    w.totalInteractions=Math.max(0,Number(w.totalInteractions)||0);
    w.lastAyah=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Number(w.lastAyah)||1));
    w.lastWordId=typeof w.lastWordId==='string'?w.lastWordId:null;
    for(const list of Object.values(WORDS)){
      for(const item of list){
        const p=w.words[item.key]=w.words[item.key]&&typeof w.words[item.key]==='object'?w.words[item.key]:{};
        p.seenCount=Math.max(0,Number(p.seenCount)||0);
        p.known=p.known===true;
        p.firstSeenAt=typeof p.firstSeenAt==='string'?p.firstSeenAt:null;
        p.lastSeenAt=typeof p.lastSeenAt==='string'?p.lastSeenAt:null;
      }
    }
    return w;
  }
  function occurrences(){
    const map={};
    for(const list of Object.values(WORDS)){
      for(const item of list){
        map[item.key]=(map[item.key]||0)+1;
      }
    }
    return map;
  }
  function uniqueKeys(){return Object.keys(ensureProgress().words)}
  function knownCount(){
    const p=ensureProgress();
    return uniqueKeys().filter(k=>p.words[k]?.known).length;
  }
  function selected(){
    const list=WORDS[view.ayah]||[];
    return list.find(x=>x.id===view.selectedId)||null;
  }
  function inspectWord(item,write=true){
    if(!item)return null;
    const p=ensureProgress();
    const now=new Date().toISOString();
    if(write){
      const row=p.words[item.key];
      row.seenCount++;
      row.firstSeenAt=row.firstSeenAt||now;
      row.lastSeenAt=now;
      p.totalInteractions++;
      p.lastAyah=view.ayah;
      p.lastWordId=item.id;
      DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    }
    return {...item,progress:{...p.words[item.key]}};
  }
  function choose(id){
    const item=(WORDS[view.ayah]||[]).find(x=>x.id===id);
    if(!item)return false;
    view.selectedId=id;
    inspectWord(item,true);
    redraw();
    return true;
  }
  function setAyah(n){
    n=Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Number(n)||1));
    view.ayah=n;
    view.selectedId=null;
    ensureProgress().lastAyah=n;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    redraw();
    return n;
  }
  function toggleKnown(){
    const item=selected();
    if(!item)return false;
    const p=ensureProgress();
    p.words[item.key].known=!p.words[item.key].known;
    DEEN_QURAN.persistState?.();
    redraw();
    return p.words[item.key].known;
  }
  function detailHtml(){
    const item=selected();
    if(!item){
      return '<div class="deen-wbw-detail"><div class="deen-wbw-gloss"><b>Bir kelime seç</b><br>Transliterasyon, bağlamsal kısa karşılık ve dil bilgisi burada görünecek.</div></div>';
    }
    const row=ensureProgress().words[item.key];
    return '<div class="deen-wbw-detail">'+
      '<div class="deen-wbw-ar" lang="ar">'+esc(item.arabic)+'</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-wbw-trans">'+esc(item.trans)+'</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-wbw-gloss"><b>BAĞLAMSAL KISA KARŞILIK</b><br>'+esc(item.gloss)+'</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-wbw-meta">'+
        '<div><strong>'+esc(item.grammar)+'</strong><small>DİL BİLGİSİ</small></div>'+
        '<div><strong>'+row.seenCount+'</strong><small>GÖRÜLME</small></div>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-wbw-actions">'+
        '<button class="deen-wbw-btn '+(row.known?'secondary':'')+'" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_WBW.toggleKnown()">'+(row.known?'ÖĞRENİLDİ ✓':'BUNU ÖĞRENDİM')+'</button>'+
        '<button class="deen-wbw-btn secondary" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_WBW.nextWord()">SONRAKİ KELİME</button>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-wbw-source">Transliterasyon/morfoloji: Quranic Arabic Corpus · Türkçe kısa karşılık: ayet bağlamı Quran.com Türkçe metniyle çapraz kontrol edilmiştir.</div>'+
    '</div>';
  }
  function frequentHtml(){
    const occ=occurrences(),p=ensureProgress();
    const rows=Object.keys(occ).filter(k=>occ[k]>1).sort((a,b)=>occ[b]-occ[a]||a.localeCompare(b));
    const label={allah:'ٱللَّهُ · Allah',ahad:'أَحَدٌ · aḥadun',walam:'وَلَمْ · wa-lam'};
    return '<div class="deen-wbw-frequent">'+
      '<h3>Bu pakette tekrar eden kelimeler</h3>'+
      '<div class="deen-wbw-frequent-list">'+
        rows.map(k=>'<div class="deen-wbw-frequent-row"><span>'+esc(label[k]||k)+'</span><b>'+occ[k]+'× · '+(p.words[k]?.known?'öğrenildi':'çalışılıyor')+'</b></div>').join('')+
      '</div>'+
    '</div>';
  }
  function html(){
    const p=ensureProgress();
    const list=WORDS[view.ayah]||[];
    const total=uniqueKeys().length;
    return '<div class="deen-wbw">'+
      '<div class="deen-wbw-head">'+
        '<span><b>Kelime Kelime İhlâs</b><small>Kelimeye dokun · transliterasyon ve kısa bağlamsal karşılığı gör</small></span>'+
        '<span class="deen-wbw-score">'+knownCount()+'/'+total+' öğrenildi</span>'+
      '</div>'+
      '<div class="deen-wbw-tabs">'+[1,2,3,4].map(n=>
        '<button class="deen-wbw-tab'+(view.ayah===n?' active':'')+'" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_WBW.setAyah('+n+')">Ayet '+n+'</button>'
      ).join('')+'</div>'+
      '<section class="deen-wbw-card">'+
        '<div class="deen-wbw-kicker">İHLÂS · '+view.ayah+'. AYET</div>'+
        '<div class="deen-wbw-verse">'+
          list.map(item=>{
            const row=p.words[item.key]||{};
            return '<button class="deen-wbw-word'+(row.known?' known':'')+(view.selectedId===item.id?' selected':'')+'" type="button" onclick="DEEN_QURAN_WBW.choose(\''+esc(item.id)+'\')">'+esc(item.arabic)+'</button>';
          }).join('')+
        '</div>'+
        detailHtml()+
      '</section>'+
      frequentHtml()+
      '<div class="deen-quran-note">Buradaki Türkçe alan tam cümle meali değildir; kelimenin bu ayetteki kısa bağlamsal karşılığıdır. Ayrıntılı anlam için ayetin doğrulanmış mealini kullan.</div>'+
    '</div>';
  }
  function redraw(){
    const mount=document.querySelector('.deen-wbw');
    if(!mount)return false;
    const holder=document.createElement('div');
    holder.innerHTML=html();
    mount.replaceWith(holder.firstElementChild);
    return true;
  }
  function open(ayah){
    const p=ensureProgress();
    view={ayah:Math.max(1,Math.min(4,Number(ayah)||p.lastAyah||1)),selectedId:null};
    sheet('Kelime Kelime Kur’an',html());
    document.documentElement.dataset.deenQuranWordByWord='v10927';
    return true;
  }
  function nextWord(){
    const list=WORDS[view.ayah]||[];
    if(!list.length)return false;
    const current=list.findIndex(x=>x.id===view.selectedId);
    const next=list[(current+1+list.length)%list.length];
    return choose(next.id);
  }
  function model(){
    const p=ensureProgress();
    return {
      ayah:view.ayah,
      selected:selected()?.id||null,
      uniqueWords:uniqueKeys().length,
      known:knownCount(),
      totalInteractions:p.totalInteractions,
      progress:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p.words))
    };
  }
  function injectDashboard(){
    const quick=document.querySelector('.deen-quran-quick');
    if(!quick||quick.querySelector('[data-quran-wordbyword]'))return false;
    const btn=document.createElement('button');
    btn.type='button';
    btn.setAttribute('data-quran-wordbyword','1');
    btn.innerHTML='<i>ك</i><b>Kelime Kelime</b><small>Transliterasyon ve anlam</small>';
    btn.onclick=()=>open();
    quick.appendChild(btn);
    return true;
  }

  window.DEEN_QURAN=window.DEEN_QURAN||{};
  window.DEEN_QURAN.wordByWord=open;
  window.DEEN_QURAN.openWordByWord=open;
  window.DEEN_QURAN_WBW={
    open,choose,setAyah,toggleKnown,nextWord,model,
    words:()=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(WORDS)),
    source:()=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(SOURCE)),
    occurrences
  };

  const oldDashboard=DEEN_QURAN.dashboardRender||DEEN_QURAN.render;
  if(typeof oldDashboard==='function'&&!oldDashboard.__deenWbwWrapped){
    const wrapped=function(){
      const out=oldDashboard.apply(this,arguments);
      injectDashboard();
      return out;
    };
    wrapped.__deenWbwWrapped=true;
    DEEN_QURAN.dashboardRender=wrapped;
    DEEN_QURAN.render=wrapped;
    window.leagueView=wrapped;
    try{leagueView=wrapped}catch(_){}
  }
  document.addEventListener('deen:navigated',()=>{
    if(document.querySelector('.screen.active')?.id==='leagueScreen')setTimeout(injectDashboard,0);
  });
  if(document.querySelector('.screen.active')?.id==='leagueScreen')setTimeout(injectDashboard,0);
})();
</script>
